 860:                 self.calibration.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
 861: 
 862:     @staticmethod
 863:     def _sync_status(sync: SyncRuntime) -> dict[str, Any]:
 864:         m = sync.sender.metrics()
 865:         return {
 866:             "pending": m.pending_total,
 867:             "pending_by_priority": m.pending,
 868:             "dead": m.dead,
 869:             "oldest_pending_age_seconds": m.oldest_pending_age_seconds,
 870:             "sent_total": m.sent_total,
 871:             "rejected_total": m.rejected_total,
 872:             "dropped_total": m.dropped_total,
 873:             "failed_sends": m.failed_sends,
 874:             "consecutive_failures": m.consecutive_failures,
 875:             "last_error": m.last_error,
 876:             "last_success_at": None if m.last_success_at is None else m.last_success_at.isoformat(),
 877:             "advisory_config": None if sync.advisory is None else sync.advisory.status(),
 878:         }
 879: 
 880:     def _calibration_version(self) -> str | None:
 881:         current = None if self.calibration is None else self.calibration.current
 882:         return None if current is None else current.version
 883: 
 884:     def requirements(self) -> ComputeRequirements:
 885:         """What the scanner computes: from the cloud's advisory config when there is one (fresh or cached),
 886:         else from the local preferences.
 887: 
 888:         Recomputed when a new ranking arrives (its top N is part of the monitored set) or the config
 889:         changes."""
 890:         run = None if self.ranking is None else self.ranking.last_run
 891:         remote = None if self.sync is None or self.sync.advisory is None else self.sync.advisory.current
 892:         key = (None if run is None else run.computed_at, None if remote is None else remote.version)
 893:         if self._requirements_cache is None or self._requirements_cache[0] != key:
 894:             ranked = [] if run is None else [r.symbol for r in run.ranked if r.eligible]
 895:             # the account cannot trade these (minimum lot or margin): not scanned until equity allows it
 896:             unaffordable = (
 897:                 []
 898:                 if run is None
 899:                 else [r.symbol for r in run.ranked if ACCOUNT_GATES & {g.value for g in r.suitability.failed}]
 900:             )
 901:             universe = [] if self.ranking is None else [e.symbol for e in self.ranking.universe]
 902:             evidence, strategies = evidence_registry(), default_registry()
 903:             available = universe or None
 904:             if remote is None:
 905:                 req = local_requirements(
 906:                     self.config,
 907:                     ranked=ranked,
 908:                     evidence=evidence,
 909:                     strategies=strategies,
 910:                     available=available,
 911:                     unaffordable=unaffordable,
 912:                 )
 913:             else:
 914:                 req = requirements_from_config(
 915:                     remote,
 916:                     self.config,
 917:                     ranked=ranked,
 918:                     evidence=evidence,
 919:                     strategies=strategies,
 920:                     available=available,
 921:                     unaffordable=unaffordable,
 922:                 )
 923:             self._requirements_cache = (key, req)
 924:         return self._requirements_cache[1]
 925: 
 926:     # --- remote commands (TAA-704) --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 927: 
 928:     def disabled_strategies(self) -> set[str]:
 929:         """Strategies a remote command disabled; persisted, re-enabled only by ``app.cli strategy enable``."""
 930:         state = EngineStateRepository(self.db, self.clock).load(DISABLED_KEY) or {}
 931:         return {str(n) for n in state.get("names", [])}
 932: 
 933:     def _drain_commands(self) -> None:
 934:         if self.commands is None or self.sync is None:
 935:             return
 936:         try:
 937:             drain(self.sync.inbox, self.commands)
 938:         except Exception as exc:  # command boundary: a broken command never stops the loop
 939:             log.exception("remote command processing failed")
 940:             self.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
 941: 
 942:     def _command_handlers(self) -> dict[CommandType, Handler]:
 943:         return {
 944:             CommandType.KILL_SWITCH_ACTIVATE: self._cmd_kill_switch,
 945:             CommandType.STRATEGY_DISABLE: self._cmd_strategy_disable,
 946:             CommandType.RESYNC: self._cmd_resync,
 947:             CommandType.RESCAN_SUITABILITY: self._cmd_rescan,
 948:             CommandType.POSITION_CLOSE: self._cmd_position_close,
 949:             CommandType.FLATTEN_ALL: self._cmd_flatten,
 950:         }
 951: 
 952:     @staticmethod
 953:     def _reason(cmd: Command, default: str) -> str:
 954:         reason = cmd.params.get("reason", default)
 955:         if not isinstance(reason, str) or not reason.strip():
 956:             raise CommandFailed("params.reason must be a non-empty string")
 957:         return reason.strip()[:200]
 958: 
 959:     def _cmd_kill_switch(self, cmd: Command) -> str:
 960:         state = self.kill_switch.activate(self._reason(cmd, "remote kill switch"), cmd.actor, "cloud")
 961:         self._check_kill_switch()
 962:         return f"kill switch active ({state.mode})"
 963: 
 964:     def _cmd_strategy_disable(self, cmd: Command) -> str:
 965:         name = cmd.params.get("strategy")
 966:         if not isinstance(name, str) or name not in self.strategy_names:
 967:             raise CommandFailed(f"unknown strategy {name!r}")
 968:         names = sorted(self.disabled_strategies() | {name})
 969:         EngineStateRepository(self.db, self.clock).save(DISABLED_KEY, {"names": names})
 970:         return f"strategy {name} disabled (re-enable locally: app.cli strategy enable)"
 971: 
 972:     def _cmd_resync(self, cmd: Command) -> str:
 973:         self.resync_requested = True  # producers re-emit their snapshots (TAA-706/707)
 974:         rows = self.replicator.snapshot(self.db) if self.replicator is not None else 0
 975:         return f"resync queued ({rows} rows)"
 976: 
 977:     def _initial_snapshot(self) -> None:
 978:         """Once per engine database and event type: queue every replicated row, including rows written before
 979:         sync was on or before a later release added their type."""
 980:         if self.replicator is None:
 981:             return
 982:         state = EngineStateRepository(self.db, self.clock)
 983:         done = set((state.load(SNAPSHOT_KEY) or {}).get("types", []))
 984:         todo = [s.event_type for s in self.replicator.specs if s.event_type not in done]
 985:         if not todo:
 986:             return
 987:         rows = self.replicator.snapshot(self.db, types=todo)
 988:         state.save(
 989:             SNAPSHOT_KEY,
 990:             {"types": sorted(done | set(todo)), "rows": rows, "at": self.clock.now_utc().isoformat()},
 991:         )
 992: 
 993:     def _cmd_rescan(self, cmd: Command) -> str:
 994:         if not self.request_rescan():
 995:             raise CommandFailed("the ranking is disabled in this engine")
 996:         return "rescan queued"
 997: 
 998:     def _cmd_position_close(self, cmd: Command) -> str:
 999:         ticket = cmd.params.get("ticket")
1000:         if not isinstance(ticket, int) or isinstance(ticket, bool):
1001:             raise CommandFailed("params.ticket must be an integer")
1002:         return self.backend.close_position(ticket, ExitReason.MANUAL)
1003: 
1004:     def _cmd_flatten(self, cmd: Command) -> str:
1005:         reason = self._reason(cmd, "remote flatten")
1006:         state = self.kill_switch.activate(
1007:             reason, cmd.actor, "cloud", KillMode.FLATTEN
1008:         )  # PermissionError if off
1009:         self._check_kill_switch()
1010:         return f"kill switch active ({state.mode}); bot positions close on the next cycle"
1011: 
1012:     def request_rescan(self) -> bool:
1013:         """The RESCAN_SUITABILITY command: the next cycle refreshes every symbol's ranking metrics."""
1014:         if self.ranking is None:
1015:             return False
1016:         self.ranking.request_rescan()
1017:         return True
1018: 
1019:     def _check_kill_switch(self) -> None:
1020:         active = self.kill_switch.is_active()
1021:         if active != self._kill_active:
1022:             self._kill_active = active
1023:             self.bus.emit(EventType.KILL_SWITCH_ACTIVATED if active else EventType.KILL_SWITCH_RELEASED)
1024: 
1025:     def _connection_changed(self, connected: bool) -> None:
1026:         if connected != self._connected:
1027:             self._connected = connected
1028:             if connected:  # positions may have closed and the clock moved while the link was down
1029:                 self._health_due.next_at = 0.0
1030:                 self._clock_due.next_at = 0.0
1031:             self.bus.emit(
1032:                 EventType.CONNECTION_RESTORED if connected else EventType.CONNECTION_LOST,
1033:                 dedupe_key="connection",
1034:             )
1035: 
1036:     def _monitor_symbol(self, symbol: str) -> None:
1037:         spec = self.symbols[symbol]
1038:         quote = self.quotes.quote(spec)
1039:         now = self.clock.now_utc()
1040:         atr = self._atr.get(symbol)
1041:         if not quote.valid and quote.problem == "no tick available":
1042:             return
1043:         self.monitor.observe_quote(
1044:             symbol,
1045:             bid=quote.bid,
1046:             ask=quote.ask,
1047:             spread_points=quote.spread_points,
1048:             spread_limit=self.config.spread_limit(symbol),
1049:             tick_age_seconds=quote.age_seconds,
1050:             in_session=self.sessions.check(symbol, now).is_open,
1051:             previous_mid=None,
1052:             atr=atr,
1053:             median_spread=self.quotes.median_spread(symbol),
1054:         )
1055:         if quote.valid:
1056:             self._last_quotes[symbol] = quote
1057:             self.backend.on_quote(symbol, quote.bid, quote.ask, atr, now)
1058:             self._quote_entries(symbol, quote.time_utc, quote.bid, quote.ask)
1059: 
1060:     @staticmethod
1061:     def _quote_signal(
1062:         strategy: OwnS2 | AITrader, ctx: StrategyContext, entry: StraddleEntry | AIEntry
1063:     ) -> Signal | None:
1064:         """The quote strategy's own signal for its own entry (each returns only its own kind)."""
1065:         if isinstance(strategy, AITrader):
1066:             return strategy.entry_signal(ctx, entry) if isinstance(entry, AIEntry) else None
1067:         return strategy.entry_signal(ctx, entry) if isinstance(entry, StraddleEntry) else None
1068: 
1069:     def _quote_entries(self, symbol: str, at: datetime, bid: float, ask: float) -> None:
1070:         """Feed the quote to the quote-driven strategies; an entry they report goes the bar path's way."""
1071:         disabled = self.disabled_strategies()
1072:         for lane, strategy in self._quote_lanes:
1073:             if not lane.trades(symbol) or strategy.name in disabled:
1074:                 continue
1075:             entry = strategy.on_quote(symbol, at, bid, ask)
1076:             if entry is None:
1077:                 continue
1078:             try:
1079:                 ctx = lane.builder.build(symbol, self.symbols[symbol])
1080:             except TaaError as exc:
1081:                 log.warning(
1082:                     "no context for %s's %s entry on %s: %s", strategy.name, entry.side.value, symbol, exc
1083:                 )
1084:                 continue
1085:             signal = self._quote_signal(strategy, ctx, entry)
1086:             if signal is not None:
1087:                 self._decide(symbol, lane, ctx, signal)
1088: 
1089:     def _stream_candles(self) -> None:
1090:         if self.candle_stream is None:
1091:             return
1092:         try:
1093:             self.candle_stream.tick(self.symbols)
1094:         except Exception:  # sync boundary: charts may lag, trading goes on
1095:             log.exception("candle stream failed")
1096:             self.candle_stream.failures += 1
1097: 
1098:     @property
1099:     def lanes(self) -> list[EntryLane]:
1100:         """The main entry timeframe (the engine's own strategies, arbiter, context), then the extra lanes."""
1101:         return [
1102:             EntryLane(MAIN, self.config, self.strategies, self.arbiter, self.builder, None),
1103:             *self.extra_lanes,
1104:         ]
1105: 
1106:     @property
1107:     def strategy_names(self) -> list[str]:
1108:         """Every strategy the engine runs: the main entry timeframe's, then each lane's."""
1109:         return [n for lane in self.lanes for n in lane.strategies.names]
1110: 
1111:     def _check_new_bar(self, symbol: str) -> None:
1112:         for lane in self.lanes:
1113:             if not lane.trades(symbol):
1114:                 continue
1115:             tf = lane.entry
1116:             frame = self.candles.closed_candles(symbol, tf, 2)
1117:             opened = frame.last_open_time
1118:             if opened is None or not self.watermarks.is_new(symbol, tf, opened):
1119:                 continue
1120:             try:
1121:                 self._evaluate(symbol, lane)
1122:             finally:
1123:                 # a bar is evaluated at most once, even when the evaluation failed (fail closed: no retry
1124:                 # storm)
1125:                 self.watermarks.mark(symbol, tf, opened)
1126: 
1127:     def _evaluate(self, symbol: str, lane: EntryLane | None = None) -> None:
1128:         lane = lane or self.lanes[0]
1129:         spec = self.symbols[symbol]
1130:         try:
1131:             ctx = lane.builder.build(symbol, spec)
1132:         except TaaError as exc:
1133:             log.warning("no context for %s (%s lane): %s", symbol, lane.name, exc)
1134:             return
1135:         if lane.name == MAIN:  # the backend's bar (paper fills, bars held) and the quote ATR: main timeframe
1136:             if ctx.market.atr is not None:
1137:                 self._atr[symbol] = ctx.market.atr
1138:             self.backend.on_bar(symbol, ctx)
1139:         # each strategy's own management; outside the main lane also its should_close, which the
1140:         # on_bar above only asks for the main lane (a bar of an extra lane never reaches it)
1141:         self.backend.on_lane_bar(symbol, ctx, lane.strategies.names, lane.name != MAIN)
1142:         self._attach_entries(symbol, lane)
1143:         disabled = self.disabled_strategies()
1144:         signals = [  # a remotely disabled strategy opens nothing; its close signals still count
1145:             s for s in lane.strategies.evaluate(ctx) if not (s.is_entry and s.strategy in disabled)
1146:         ]
1147:         selected = lane.arbiter.arbitrate(signals).selected
1148:         self.on_arbitrated(lane)
1149:         if selected is not None:
1150:             self._decide(symbol, lane, ctx, selected)
1151: 
1152:     def _attach_entries(self, symbol: str, lane: EntryLane) -> None:
1153:         """Strategies with an ``entry_group`` read the group's accepted entries of the last day from the
1154:         decision store (not from memory: the rule holds across a restart)."""
1155:         since = self.clock.now_utc() - ENTRY_LOOKBACK
1156:         for strategy in lane.strategies.strategies:
1157:             if strategy.entry_group:
1158:                 strategy.attach_entries(
1159:                     symbol, self.decision_store.accepted_entries(strategy.entry_group, symbol, since)
1160:                 )
1161: 
1162:     def _decide(self, symbol: str, lane: EntryLane, ctx: StrategyContext, selected: Signal) -> None:
1163:         """The decision engine on one selected signal, and the order when it is accepted."""
1164:         spec = self.symbols[symbol]
1165:         applied = None if self.risk_limits is None else self.risk_limits.current()
1166:         record = self.decisions.decide(
1167:             DecisionRequest(
1168:                 signal=selected,
1169:                 market=ctx.market,
1170:                 spec=spec,
1171:                 quote=self.quotes.quote(spec),
1172:                 account=AccountState(
1173:                     self.backend.funds(),
1174:                     self._book(),
1175:                     self._loss_status(),
1176:                     self._pending_orders(),
1177:                     self.backend.position_groups(),
1178:                 ),
1179:                 health=self.health_snapshot(),
1180:                 specs=self.symbols,
1181:                 gate=self.gate(),
1182:                 last_entry_at=self._last_entry.get(symbol),
1183:                 profile_limits=None if applied is None else applied.limits,
1184:                 risk_source=None if applied is None else applied.source,
1185:                 cage=None if applied is None else applied.cage,
1186:                 probation=self._probation(),
1187:             )
1188:         )
1189:         if record.decision is Decision.ACCEPT:
1190:             magic = self.magic[selected.strategy]
1191:             if self.backend.place(record, spec, magic):
1192:                 self._last_entry[symbol] = ctx.decision_time_utc
1193:                 self.on_entry(symbol, ctx.decision_time_utc)
1194:                 self.bus.emit(
1195:                     EventType.SIGNAL_ACCEPTED,
1196:                     symbol=symbol,
1197:                     strategy=selected.strategy,
1198:                     side=selected.action.value,
1199:                     volume=str(record.volume),
1200:                     decision_id=record.decision_id,
1201:                     paper=self.settings.mode is TradingMode.PAPER,
1202:                 )
1203: 
1204:     def on_arbitrated(self, lane: EntryLane | None = None) -> None:
1205:         lane = lane or self.lanes[0]
1206:         self.state.save(lane.state_key, lane.arbiter.cooldown_state())
1207: 
1208:     def on_entry(self, symbol: str, at: datetime) -> None:
1209:         self.state.save("last_entry", {sym: t.isoformat() for sym, t in self._last_entry.items()})
1210: 
1211:     def _scan_limits(self) -> ProfileLimits | None:
1212:         """The owner's limits for the scanner's ADVISORY decisions: the owner's sizing and account limits, but
1213:         not min RR, a HARD check there that would hide opportunities other users' profiles accept, and not
1214:         the entry plan (alerts carry each user's own plan, TAA-1207); always STANDARD (PLAN Â§A36)."""
1215:         if self.risk_limits is None:
1216:             return None
1217:         return dataclasses.replace(self.risk_limits.scan_limits(), min_risk_reward=None, entry_plan=None)
1218: 
1219:     def effective_risk(self) -> RiskConfig:
1220:         """The limits the engine trades with now (TAA-710); ``config.yaml`` alone before ``start()``."""
1221:         return self.config.risk if self.risk_limits is None else self.risk_limits.current().effective
1222: 
1223:     def _pending_orders(self) -> list[BrokerOrder]:
1224:         try:
1225:             return self.backend.pending_orders()
1226:         except TaaError:
1227:             log.warning("could not read resting orders for exposure")
1228:             return []
1229: 
1230:     def _book(self) -> list[BrokerPosition]:
1231:         try:
1232:             return self.backend.book()
1233:         except TaaError:
1234:             log.warning("could not read positions for exposure")
1235:             return []
1236: 
1237:     def _loss_status(self) -> LossStatus:
1238:         # the whole bot range, not only today's strategies: a disabled strategy's open trade still counts
1239:         base = self.settings.env.MAGIC_NUMBER_BASE
1240:         return self.losses.observe(
1241:             self.backend.equity(),
1242:             self.backend.new_deals(),
1243:             is_bot=lambda d: base <= d.magic < base + MAGIC_RANGE,
1244:         )
1245: 
1246:     def _health(self) -> None:
1247:         self._broker_step("loss tracking", self._observe_losses)
1248:         self.board.tick()
1249:         write_ok = self.db.healthcheck()
1250:         data_dir = self.settings.path("data")
1251:         free_gb = shutil.disk_usage(data_dir).free / 1e9 if data_dir.exists() else None
1252:         self.monitor.observe_storage(write_ok, free_gb)
1253:         self._broker_step("broker maintenance", self.backend.maintain)
1254:         self.heartbeat()
1255: 
1256:     def _observe_losses(self) -> None:
1257:         status = self._loss_status()
1258:         self._last_loss = status
1259:         self.monitor.risk = self._breaker_risk()
1260:         self.monitor.observe_losses(status)
1261: 
1262:     def _breaker_risk(self) -> RiskConfig:
1263:         """The limits the loss breakers trip on: ``config.yaml`` â†’ ``risk``, or in the aggressive preset its
1264:         cage's (daily 2r, weekly 35 %, drawdown kill 60 %; PLAN Â§A36). Leaving the preset returns to
1265:         ``risk:``, where a deeper drawdown latches MAX_DRAWDOWN (``app.cli risk rebase-hwm`` after a
1266:         review)."""
1267:         applied = None if self.risk_limits is None else self.risk_limits.current()
1268:         return applied.effective if applied is not None and applied.aggressive else self.config.risk
1269: 
1270:     def _broker_step(self, what: str, step: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
1271:         """Run a health step that reads the broker. A lost terminal link is a connection problem, not an
1272:         unhandled one (2026-10-09: "No IPC connection" after a laptop sleep tripped UNHANDLED_EXCEPTION three
1273:         times and latched it): when the link probe fails too, skip the step; the loss status keeps its last
1274:         value until the link is back."""
1275:         try:
1276:             step()
1277:         except BrokerError as exc:
1278:             if self.bundle.client.is_healthy():
1279:                 raise  # the link is fine: a real failure, UNHANDLED_EXCEPTION as before
1280:             log.warning("%s skipped: the terminal link is down (%s)", what, exc)
1281: 
1282:     def heartbeat(self, state: str = "running") -> None:
1283:         write_heartbeat(self.heartbeat_path, self.clock.now_utc(), self.status(), state=state)
1284: 
1285:     def _cloud_heartbeat(self, now: float) -> None:
1286:         if self.heartbeats is None:
1287:             return
1288:         try:
1289:             self.heartbeats.maybe_emit(now, self.cloud_heartbeat)
1290:         except Exception:  # telemetry boundary: a heartbeat problem never touches trading
1291:             log.exception("cloud heartbeat failed")
1292: 
1293:     def cloud_heartbeat(self, state: str = "running") -> dict[str, Any]:
1294:         """The heartbeat sent to the cloud (TAA-705): liveness, counters, quotes and the market schedule."""
1295:         now = self.clock.now_utc()
1296:         market_open, change_at = market_state(
1297:             self.symbols.values(), now, self.config.advisory.sessions.overrides
1298:         )
1299:         backend = getattr(self, "backend", None)
1300:         quotes = [
1301:             {
1302:                 "symbol": q.symbol,
1303:                 "bid": q.bid,
1304:                 "ask": q.ask,
1305:                 "spread_points": q.spread_points if math.isfinite(q.spread_points) else None,
1306:                 "max_spread_points": self.config.spread_limit(q.symbol),
1307:                 "time": q.time_utc,
1308:             }
1309:             for q in sorted(self._last_quotes.values(), key=lambda q: q.symbol)
1310:         ][:MAX_QUOTES]
1311:         return {
1312:             "at": now,
1313:             "run_id": self.run_id,
1314:             "mode": self.settings.mode.value,
1315:             "state": state,
1316:             "connected": self._connected,
1317:             "clock_verified": self._clock_ok,
1318:             "kill_switch": self._kill_active,
1319:             "open_positions": 0 if backend is None else backend.open_positions(),
1320:             "cycles": self.cycles,
1321:             "market_open": market_open,
1322:             "market_change_at": change_at,
1323:             "outbox_pending": None if self.sync is None else self.sync.sender.metrics().pending_total,
1324:             "account": self._account_snapshot(),
1325:             "disabled_strategies": sorted(self.disabled_strategies()),
1326:             "quotes": quotes,
1327:             "forming": self._forming_bars(),
1328:         }
1329: 
1330:     def _forming_bars(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
1331:         """The forming bar of every traded symbol on every streamed timeframe, for the chart's live candle."""
1332:         if self.candle_stream is None or not self._connected:
1333:             return []
1334:         out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
1335:         for symbol in sorted(self.symbols):
1336:             for tf in self.candle_stream.timeframes:
1337:                 try:
1338:                     bar = self.candles.forming_bar(symbol, tf)
1339:                 except TaaError:  # a broker hiccup costs this heartbeat one bar, nothing more
1340:                     continue
1341:                 if bar is None:
1342:                     continue
1343:                 out.append(
1344:                     {
1345:                         "symbol": symbol,
1346:                         "timeframe": tf.value,
1347:                         "open_time": ensure_utc(bar["open_time"].to_pydatetime()),
1348:                         "open": float(bar["open"]),
1349:                         "high": float(bar["high"]),
1350:                         "low": float(bar["low"]),
1351:                         "close": float(bar["close"]),
1352:                         "tick_volume": int(bar["tick_volume"]),
1353:                     }
1354:                 )
1355:         return out[:MAX_FORMING]
1356: 
1357:     def _account_snapshot(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
1358:         """The traded account for the dashboard (TAA-904), from the last health step's loss status plus the
1359:         current funds and exposure. Telemetry only: any failure sends no snapshot instead of a wrong one."""
1360:         status, backend = self._last_loss, getattr(self, "backend", None)
1361:         if status is None or backend is None:
1362:             return None
1363:         try:
1364:             funds = backend.funds()
1365:             manager = ExposureManager(
1366:                 self.config.risk, self.decisions.calculator, magic_base=self.settings.env.MAGIC_NUMBER_BASE
1367:             )
1368:             book = self._book()
1369:             exposure = manager.snapshot(
1370:                 book,
1371:                 funds,
1372:                 self.decisions.position_specs(self.symbols, book),
1373:                 pending=self._pending_orders(),
1374:                 groups=backend.position_groups(),
1375:             )
1376:         except Exception:  # telemetry boundary
1377:             log.exception("account snapshot for the heartbeat failed")
1378:             return None
1379:         applied = None if self.risk_limits is None else self.risk_limits.current()
1380:         risk = self.config.risk if applied is None else applied.effective
1381:         foreign = exposure.foreign[:MAX_FOREIGN_POSITIONS]
1382:         try:
1383:             links = self.manual_links.observe([p.position for p in foreign])
1384:             open_ids = [p.position.identifier or p.position.ticket for p in exposure.foreign]
1385:             self.manual_links.settle(open_ids, self.gateway.deals)
1386:             base = self.settings.env.MAGIC_NUMBER_BASE
1387:             # manual trades that opened and closed while the engine did not watch (stopped, or in between)
1388:             self.manual_links.backfill(
1389:                 open_ids, self.gateway.deals, lambda magic: base <= magic < base + MAGIC_RANGE
1390:             )
1391:         except Exception:  # telemetry boundary: an unlinked position is still shown
1392:             log.exception("manual trade links failed")
1393:             links = {}
1394: 
1395:         def num(value: float, digits: int = 2) -> float | None:
1396:             return round(value, digits) if math.isfinite(value) else None
1397: 
1398:         return {
1399:             "as_of": status.as_of,
1400:             "backend": backend.name,
1401:             "currency": self._currency,
1402:             "balance": num(funds.balance),
1403:             "equity": num(funds.equity),
1404:             "margin": num(funds.margin),
1405:             "margin_free": num(funds.margin_free),
1406:             "day_pnl": num(status.day_pnl),
1407:             "day_pnl_percent": num(status.day_pnl_percent, 4),
1408:             "week_pnl": num(status.week_pnl),
1409:             "week_pnl_percent": num(status.week_pnl_percent, 4),
1410:             "drawdown_percent": num(status.drawdown_percent, 4),
1411:             "open_risk": num(exposure.open_risk),
1412:             "heat_percent": num(exposure.heat_percent, 4),
1413:             "unknown_risk_positions": len(exposure.unknown_risk),
1414:             "consecutive_losses": status.consecutive_losses,
1415:             "effective_leverage": num(exposure.effective_leverage),
1416:             "max_effective_leverage": risk.max_effective_leverage,
1417:             "broker_account": self._broker_account() if backend.name == "paper" else None,
1418:             "bot_positions": None if backend.name == "paper" else self._bot_positions(exposure),
1419:             "foreign_positions": [
1420:                 foreign_position(
1421:                     p,
1422:                     counted=risk.foreign_positions_policy == "count",
1423:                     link=links.get(p.position.identifier or p.position.ticket),
1424:                 )
1425:                 for p in foreign
1426:             ],
1427:             "limits": {
1428:                 "daily_loss_percent": risk.max_daily_loss_percent,
1429:                 "weekly_loss_percent": risk.max_weekly_loss_percent,
1430:                 "drawdown_percent": risk.max_account_drawdown_percent,
1431:                 "heat_percent": risk.max_total_open_risk_percent,
1432:                 "consecutive_losses": risk.max_consecutive_losses,
1433:             },
1434:             "risk_limits": None if self.risk_limits is None else self.risk_limits.snapshot(),
1435:         }
1436: 
1437:     def _bot_positions(self, exposure: Exposure) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
1438:         """The bot's open broker positions for the heartbeat (TAA-1211), as MT5 reports them now."""
1439:         base = self.settings.env.MAGIC_NUMBER_BASE
1440:         by_magic = {magic: name for name, magic in self.magic.items()}
1441:         initial = self.broker_positions.initial_stops() if hasattr(self, "broker_positions") else {}
1442:         mine = [p for p in exposure.positions if base <= p.position.magic < base + MAGIC_RANGE]
1443:         return [
1444:             bot_position(
1445:                 p, strategy=by_magic.get(p.position.magic), sl_initial=initial.get(p.position.ticket)
1446:             )
1447:             for p in mine[:MAX_FOREIGN_POSITIONS]
1448:         ]
1449: 
1450:     def _broker_account(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
1451:         """The real MT5 account's figures while the bot trades a PAPER book (None if unreadable)."""
1452:         try:
1453:             a = self.gateway.account()
1454:         except TaaError:
1455:             return None
1456:         return {
1457:             "currency": a.currency,
1458:             "balance": a.balance,
1459:             "equity": a.equity,
1460:             "margin_free": a.margin_free,
1461:             "leverage": a.leverage,
1462:         }
1463: 
1464:     def _final_cloud_heartbeat(self) -> None:
1465:         """A deliberate stop queues ``state: stopped``, so the cloud's watchdog alerts at once."""
1466:         if self.heartbeats is None:
1467:             return
1468:         try:
1469:             self.heartbeats.emit(self.cloud_heartbeat("stopped"))
1470:         except Exception:  # shutdown boundary
1471:             log.exception("final cloud heartbeat failed")
1472: 
1473:     def _verify_clock(self, *, initial: bool = False) -> None:
1474:         cfg = self.config
1475:         symbol, result = verify_server_time_any(
1476:             self.gateway, cfg.symbols.clock_symbols, wait_seconds=10.0 if initial else 5.0, sleep=self.sleep
1477:         )
1478:         self._apply_clock(result, symbol)
1479: 
1480:     def _apply_clock(self, result: ClockVerification, symbol: str | None) -> None:
1481:         if result.status is ClockStatus.UNVERIFIED_MARKET_IDLE:
1482:             log.info("server time not verifiable now (idle markets); keeping the previous state")
1483:             return  # no verdict: the previous verification stands, and without one entries stay blocked
1484:         self._clock_ok = result.ok
1485:         if hasattr(self, "monitor"):
1486:             self.monitor.observe_clock(result.ok, result.detail)
1487:         if not result.ok:
1488:             self.bus.emit(EventType.CLOCK_UNVERIFIED, detail=result.detail, symbol=symbol, dedupe_key="clock")
1489: 
1490:     def health_snapshot(self) -> SystemHealth:
1491:         return SystemHealth(
1492:             kill_switch_active=self._kill_active,
1493:             broker_healthy=self._connected,
1494:             storage_healthy=True,
1495:             clock_verified=self._clock_ok,
1496:         )
1497: 
1498:     def status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
1499:         """For the health endpoint and the heartbeat (TAA-605)."""
1500:         backend = getattr(self, "backend", None)
1501:         try:
1502:             funds = None if backend is None else backend.funds()
1503:         except TaaError:
1504:             funds = None
1505:         return {
1506:             "run_id": self.run_id,
1507:             "mode": self.settings.mode.value,
1508:             "running": self.running,
1509:             "cycles": self.cycles,
1510:             "connected": self._connected,
1511:             "clock_verified": self._clock_ok,
1512:             "kill_switch": self._kill_active,
1513:             "symbols": sorted(self.symbols),
1514:             "backend": None if backend is None else backend.name,
1515:             "open_positions": 0 if backend is None else backend.open_positions(),
1516:             "equity": None if funds is None else round(funds.equity, 2),
1517:             "breakers": [
1518:                 {"name": s.name.value, "scope": s.scope_key, "state": s.state.value}
1519:                 for s in (self.board.blocking() if hasattr(self, "board") else [])
1520:             ],
1521:             "last_error": self.last_error,
1522:             "scanner": None
1523:             if self.scanner is None
1524:             else {
1525:                 "bars": self.scanner.stats.bars,
1526:                 "opportunities": self.scanner.stats.opportunities,
1527:                 "hidden": self.scanner.stats.hidden,
1528:                 "pending": len(self.scanner.pending),
1529:                 "failures": self.scanner.stats.failures,
1530:                 "last_duration_ms": round(self.scanner.stats.last_duration_ms),
1531:                 "last_error": self.scanner.stats.last_error,
1532:             },
1533:             "shadow": None
1534:             if self.shadow is None
1535:             else {
1536:                 "opened": self.shadow.stats.opened,
1537:                 "closed": self.shadow.stats.closed,
1538:                 "failures": self.shadow.stats.failures,
1539:                 "last_duration_ms": round(self.shadow.stats.last_duration_ms),
1540:                 "last_error": self.shadow.stats.last_error,
1541:             },
1542:             "ai_notes": None
1543:             if self.ai_notes is None
1544:             else {
1545:                 "calls": self.ai_notes.stats.calls,
1546:                 "answered": self.ai_notes.stats.answered,
1547:                 "failures": self.ai_notes.stats.failures,
1548:                 "budget_holds": self.ai_notes.stats.budget_holds,
1549:                 "last_error": self.ai_notes.stats.last_error,
1550:             },
1551:             "sync": None if self.sync is None else self._sync_status(self.sync),
1552:             "candle_stream": None
1553:             if self.candle_stream is None
1554:             else {"events": self.candle_stream.events, "failures": self.candle_stream.failures},
1555:             "replication": None
1556:             if self.replicator is None
1557:             else {"emitted": self.replicator.emitted, "errors": self.replicator.errors},
1558:             "calibration": None
1559:             if self.calibration is None
1560:             else {
1561:                 "version": None if self.calibration.current is None else self.calibration.current.version,
1562:                 "builds": self.calibration.builds,
1563:                 "failures": self.calibration.failures,
1564:                 "last_error": self.calibration.last_error,
1565:             },
1566:             "ranking": None
1567:             if self.ranking is None
1568:             else {
1569:                 "runs": self.ranking.stats.runs,
1570:                 "symbols": len(self.ranking.cache),
1571:                 "refreshed": self.ranking.stats.refreshed,
1572:                 "failures": self.ranking.stats.failures,
1573:                 "last_duration_ms": round(self.ranking.stats.last_duration_ms),
1574:                 "last_error": self.ranking.stats.last_error,
1575:             },
1576:         }
1577: 
1578:     # --- shutdown ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1579: 
1580:     def shutdown(self, *, failed: bool = False) -> None:
1581:         """*failed*: the run ended on an error (see :meth:`run`). The heartbeat file then says ``failed``
1582:         instead of ``stopped``, so ``scripts/watchdog.ps1`` and ``demo-tasks.ps1 -Check`` restart the engine
1583:         rather than leave it down as a deliberate stop."""
1584:         self.running = False
1585:         try:
1586:             if self.calibration is not None:
1587:                 self.calibration.shutdown()
1588:             if self.ai_notes is not None:
1589:                 self.ai_notes.close()
1590:             if self.ai_trader is not None:
1591:                 self.ai_trader.close()
1592:             if self.sync is not None:
1593:                 self._final_cloud_heartbeat()
1594:                 self.sync.stop(final_flush=True)  # a deliberate stop reaches the cloud at once
1595:             if hasattr(self, "backend") and isinstance(self.backend, PaperBackend):
1596:                 self.backend.maintain()
1597:             # "stopped": a deliberate stop the watchdogs leave alone; "failed": they restart the engine
1598:             self.heartbeat("failed" if failed else "stopped")
1599:             if failed:
1600:                 log.error("engine stopped after a failure (%s): heartbeat says failed", self.last_error)
1601:             self.runs.finish(self.run_id, "STOPPED", self.last_error)
1602:             self.audit.append(
1603:                 "ENGINE_STOP", self.process, {"run_id": self.run_id, "cycles": self.cycles, "failed": failed}
1604:             )
1605:             self.bus.emit(EventType.ENGINE_STOPPED, cycles=self.cycles, failed=failed)
1606:         finally:
1607:             self.bundle.client.shutdown()
1608: 
1609: 
1610: def bot_position(measured: PositionRisk, *, strategy: str | None, sl_initial: float | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
1611:     """One of the bot's own broker positions for the heartbeat (``BotPosition``)."""
1612:     p = measured.position
1613: 
1614:     def num(value: float) -> float | None:
1615:         return value if math.isfinite(value) else None
1616: 
1617:     return {
1618:         "ticket": p.ticket,
1619:         "symbol": p.symbol,
1620:         "side": p.side.value,
1621:         "volume": p.volume,
1622:         "price_open": p.price_open,
1623:         "price_current": p.price_current,
1624:         "sl": p.sl if p.sl > 0 else None,
1625:         "tp": p.tp if p.tp > 0 else None,
1626:         "sl_initial": sl_initial,
1627:         "profit": num(p.profit) or 0.0,
1628:         "swap": num(p.swap) or 0.0,
1629:         "opened_at": p.time_utc,
1630:         "magic": p.magic,
1631:         "strategy": strategy,
1632:         "risk_to_stop": None if measured.risk_to_stop is None else round(measured.risk_to_stop, 2),
1633:     }
1634: 
1635: 
1636: def foreign_position(
1637:     measured: PositionRisk, *, counted: bool, link: ManualTradeLinkRow | None = None
1638: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
1639:     """A manual position for the heartbeat (``ForeignPosition``): what MT5 reports plus its risk to stop."""
1640:     p = measured.position
1641: 
1642:     def num(value: float) -> float | None:
1643:         return value if math.isfinite(value) else None
1644: 
1645:     return {
1646:         "ticket": p.ticket,
1647:         "symbol": p.symbol,
1648:         "side": p.side.value,
1649:         "volume": p.volume,
1650:         "price_open": p.price_open,
1651:         "price_current": p.price_current,
1652:         "sl": p.sl if p.sl > 0 else None,
1653:         "tp": p.tp if p.tp > 0 else None,
1654:         "profit": num(p.profit) or 0.0,
1655:         "swap": num(p.swap) or 0.0,
1656:         "opened_at": p.time_utc,
1657:         "magic": p.magic,
1658:         "comment": p.comment[:64],
1659:         "risk_to_stop": None if measured.risk_to_stop is None else round(measured.risk_to_stop, 2),
1660:         "counted": counted,
1661:         "link": None if link is None else link_dict(link),
1662:     }

  54: ) -> tuple[ManualClock, PaperExecution, PositionManager, MemorySink]:
  55:     clock = ManualClock(NOW)

  83:         clock.advance(10)

  86:         assert position(p).stop_kind is ExitReason.TRAILING_STOP

  89:         with db.session() as sess:

  91:             assert (row.sl, row.stop_kind) == (pytest.approx(1.10210), "TRAIL")

 122:         # Found in review 2026-10-27: should_close lives in on_bar, which the orchestrator calls only for
 123:         # the main lane, so an extra lane's daily flatten never fired. on_lane_bar now asks as well - but
 124:         # only when the caller says so, or the main lane would ask twice and close twice.
 125:         clock, p, pm, _ = setup(db, record)

  19: MAGIC = 7_310_000
  20: ATR = 0.0005
  21: 
  22: 
  23: @pytest.fixture
  24: def rig(tmp_path: Path, db: Database) -> Rig:
  25:     return make_rig(tmp_path, db)
  26: 
  27: 
  28: def manager(rig: Rig, cfg: PositionManagementConfig | None = None, **kw: object) -> BrokerPositionManager:
  29:     spec = rig.bundle.gateway.symbol_spec("EURUSD")
  30:     base: dict[str, object] = {
  31:         "entry_timeframe": Timeframe.M15,
  32:         "magic_base": MAGIC,
  33:         "strategies_by_magic": {},
  34:         "flatten_allowed": True,
  35:         "bus": rig.om.bus,
  36:     }
  37:     base.update(kw)
  38:     return BrokerPositionManager(
  39:         rig.om, cfg or PositionManagementConfig(), {"EURUSD": spec}, rig.clock, **base
  40:     )  # type: ignore[arg-type]
  41: 
  42: 
  43: def opened(rig: Rig) -> float:
  44:     """Open a BUY (2 pips stop) and pin the price at its entry; returns the entry price."""
  45:     execute(rig)
  46:     [pos] = rig.bundle.gateway.positions()
  47:     rig.fake.price_override["EURUSD"] = pos.price_open
  48:     return pos.price_open
  49: 
  50: 
  51: def quote(rig: Rig, bid: float) -> None:
  52:     rig.fake.price_override["EURUSD"] = bid
  53: 
  54: 
  55: def tick(rig: Rig) -> tuple[float, float]:
  56:     t = rig.bundle.gateway.tick("EURUSD")
  57:     assert t is not None
  58:     return t.bid, t.ask
  59: 
  60: 
  61: def sl(rig: Rig) -> float:
  62:     return rig.bundle.gateway.positions()[0].sl
  63: 
  64: 
  65: class TestStopManagement:
  66:     def test_break_even_then_trailing_with_rate_limit(self, rig: Rig) -> None:
  67:         entry = opened(rig)
  68:         mgr = manager(rig)
  69:         quote(rig, entry + 0.0021)  # past +1R
  70:         mgr.on_quote("EURUSD", *tick(rig), ATR)
  71:         assert sl(rig) == pytest.approx(entry + 2 * 0.00001)
  72:         quote(rig, entry + 0.0035)  # past +1.5R, but right after the last change
  73:         mgr.on_quote("EURUSD", *tick(rig), ATR)
  74:         assert sl(rig) == pytest.approx(entry + 2 * 0.00001)
  75:         rig.clock.advance(10)
  76:         mgr.on_quote("EURUSD", *tick(rig), ATR)
  77:         assert sl(rig) == pytest.approx(entry + 0.0035 - 2 * ATR)
  78:         assert [e.type for e in rig.sink.events].count(EventType.STOP_MOVED) == 2

  20: 
  21: LANE = "setup_breakout_scalp"
  22: 
  23: 
  24: class BuyEveryM5Bar(BuyEveryBar):
  25:     name = LANE
  26: 
  27: 
  28: def lane_config(time_stop_bars: int = 3) -> LaneConfig:
  29:     return LaneConfig(
  30:         name="fast",
  31:         timeframes=TimeframesConfig(entry=Timeframe.M5, higher=Timeframe.H1),
  32:         symbols=["EURUSD"],
  33:         strategies=StrategiesConfig(items=[StrategyEntry(name=LANE)], cooldown_bars=0),
  34:         max_spread_to_sl_ratio=1.0,
  35:         management={
  36:             LANE: StrategyManagement(break_even=False, trailing=False, time_stop_bars=time_stop_bars)
  37:         },
  38:     )
  39: 
  40: 
  41: def lane_demo(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, time_stop_bars: int = 3) -> Demo:
  42:     import tests.integration.test_engine_demo as m
  43: 
  44:     base = demo_settings(tmp_path)
  45:     with_lane = dataclasses.replace(
  46:         base, config=base.config.model_copy(update={"lanes": [lane_config(time_stop_bars)]})
  47:     )
  48:     monkeypatch.setattr(m, "demo_settings", lambda tmp, **env: with_lane)
  49:     d = demo(tmp_path)
  50:     d.engine.start()
  51:     d.engine.strategies = StrategySet(())  # the main lane opens nothing: only the lane trades
  52:     d.engine.extra_lanes[0].strategies = StrategySet((BuyEveryM5Bar(BuyEveryM5Bar.Params()),))
  53:     return d
  54: 
  55: 
  56: def test_a_lane_trades_on_its_own_timeframe_with_its_own_magic(
  57:     tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
  58: ) -> None:
  59:     d = lane_demo(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
  60:     assert [lane.name for lane in d.engine.lanes] == [MAIN, "fast"]
  61:     assert d.engine.strategy_names[-1] == LANE
  62:     run_until(d, EventType.POSITION_OPENED)
  63:     with d.db.session() as sess:
  64:         row = (
  65:             sess.execute(select(DecisionRecordRow).where(DecisionRecordRow.decision == "ACCEPT"))
  66:             .scalars()
  67:             .first()
  68:         )
  69:     assert row is not None and row.strategy == LANE and row.timeframe == "M5"
  70:     (pos,) = d.engine.gateway.positions()
  71:     assert pos.magic == d.engine.magic[LANE]
  72:     d.engine.shutdown()
  73: 
  74: 
  75: def test_the_lane_time_stop_counts_the_lanes_bars(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
  76:     d = lane_demo(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
  77:     run_until(d, EventType.POSITION_OPENED)
  78:     d.engine.extra_lanes[0].strategies = StrategySet(())  # no new entries after the first
  79:     for _ in range(60):  # 3 M5 bars are 15 minutes: 30 s cycles, well within 30 minutes
  80:         d.engine.cycle()
  81:         d.clock.advance(30)
  82:         if not d.engine.gateway.positions():
  83:             break
  84:     assert d.engine.gateway.positions() == []
  85:     closed = [e for e in d.sink.events if e.type is EventType.POSITION_CLOSED]
  86:     assert [e.params.get("reason") for e in closed] == ["TIME"]  # within 30 minutes: 3 M15 bars would take 45
  87:     d.engine.shutdown()
  88: 
  89: 
  90: class CloseEveryM5Bar(BuyEveryM5Bar):
  91:     """Opens nothing and asks to close on a bar of its OWN lane only. The main lane's on_bar asks every
  92:     position's strategy, this lane's included, so a strategy that always says yes would be closed by the
  93:     main lane and prove nothing. M5 is this lane's entry frame; the main lane runs on M15."""
  94: 
  95:     def evaluate(self, ctx):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
  96:         return self.hold(ctx)
  97: 
  98:     def should_close(self, ctx, side) -> bool:  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
  99:         return ctx.market.entry_timeframe is Timeframe.M5
 100: 
 101: 
 102: def test_a_lane_strategy_closes_its_own_position(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
 103:     """Found in review 2026-10-27: ``should_close`` is asked in ``on_bar``, which the engine calls only for
 104:     the main lane, so a lane strategy's own exit (the three bots' daily flatten) never fired live although
 105:     the backtest engine honoured it. The time stop is set far out of reach, so only the strategy's own
 106:     exit can close this position, and the reason must be SIGNAL."""
 107:     d = lane_demo(tmp_path, monkeypatch, time_stop_bars=999)
 108:     run_until(d, EventType.POSITION_OPENED)
 109:     closer = CloseEveryM5Bar(CloseEveryM5Bar.Params())
 110:     d.engine.extra_lanes[0].strategies = StrategySet((closer,))
 111:     # the position manager keeps its own magic -> strategy snapshot from start-up
 112:     d.engine.broker_positions.strategies_by_magic[d.engine.magic[LANE]] = closer
 113:     for _ in range(60):  # the time stop test needs 60 cycles for three M5 bars
 114:         d.engine.cycle()
 115:         d.clock.advance(30)
 116:         if not d.engine.gateway.positions():
 117:             break
 118:     assert d.engine.gateway.positions() == []
 119:     closed = [e for e in d.sink.events if e.type is EventType.POSITION_CLOSED]
 120:     assert [e.params.get("reason") for e in closed] == ["SIGNAL"]
 121:     d.engine.shutdown()

===== tests/unit/test_evidence_zigzag.py =====
   1: from __future__ import annotations
   2: 
   3: from itertools import pairwise
   4: 
   5: import numpy as np
   6: import pandas as pd
   7: import pytest
   8: from hypothesis import given, settings
   9: from hypothesis import strategies as st
  10: 
  11: from app.core.errors import ConfigError
  12: from app.evidence.zigzag import zigzag
  13: from app.indicators.price_action import SwingKind, label_structure
  14: from app.indicators.volatility import atr
  15: from tests.indicator_data import mutate_after, random_ohlc
  16: 
  17: 
  18: def run(highs: list[float], multiple: float = 2.0) -> list[tuple[SwingKind, int, int, float]]:
  19:     h = pd.Series(highs, dtype=float)
  20:     out = zigzag(h, h - 0.5, pd.Series(np.ones(len(h))), multiple)
  21:     return [(p.kind, p.pivot_pos, p.confirm_pos, p.price) for p in out]
  22: 
  23: 
  24: def test_hand_computed_reference() -> None:
  25:     # ATR = 1, multiple 2 -> a 2.0 move against the running extreme confirms it
  26:     assert run([10, 11, 12, 11, 9.5, 9, 10, 12, 11]) == [
  27:         (SwingKind.LOW, 0, 2, 9.5),  # 12 - 9.5 >= 2 at bar 2
  28:         (SwingKind.HIGH, 2, 4, 12.0),  # 12 - 9.0 >= 2 at bar 4
  29:         (SwingKind.LOW, 5, 7, 8.5),  # 12 - 8.5 >= 2 at bar 7
  30:     ]
  31: 
  32: 
  33: def test_running_extreme_is_never_a_pivot() -> None:
  34:     # a steady rise never reverses: the low that started it is the only pivot
  35:     pivots = run([float(x) for x in range(10, 30)])
  36:     assert pivots == [(SwingKind.LOW, 0, 2, 9.5)]
  37: 
  38: 
  39: def test_no_reversal_before_atr_warmup() -> None:
  40:     h = pd.Series([10, 14, 9, 14, 9.0])
  41:     a = pd.Series([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, 1.0, 1.0])
  42:     out = zigzag(h, h - 0.5, a, 2.0)
  43:     assert out and min(p.confirm_pos for p in out) >= 3
  44: 
  45: 
  46: def test_invalid_multiple() -> None:
  47:     h = pd.Series([1.0, 2.0])
  48:     with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
  49:         zigzag(h, h, h, 0.0)
  50: 
  51: 
  52: def test_pivot_labels_are_close_times_and_feed_structure() -> None:
  53:     df = random_ohlc(400, seed=8)
  54:     a = atr(df["high"], df["low"], df["close"])
  55:     out = zigzag(df["high"], df["low"], a, 1.5)
  56:     assert len(out) > 10
  57:     assert all(p.pivot_at == df.index[p.pivot_pos] for p in out)
  58:     labels = label_structure(out)
  59:     assert {lab.label for lab in labels[2:]} <= {"HH", "LH", "HL", "LL", "EQH", "EQL"}
  60: 
  61: 
  62: def test_larger_degree_is_coarser() -> None:
  63:     df = random_ohlc(600, seed=8)
  64:     a = atr(df["high"], df["low"], df["close"])
  65:     counts = [len(zigzag(df["high"], df["low"], a, m)) for m in (1.5, 3.0, 6.0)]
  66:     assert counts[0] > counts[1] > counts[2] > 0
  67: 
  68: 
  69: @pytest.mark.parametrize("t", [120, 333, 598])
  70: def test_prefix_and_mutation_consistency(t: int) -> None:
  71:     df = random_ohlc(600, seed=8)
  72: 
  73:     def pivots(d: pd.DataFrame) -> list[tuple[object, ...]]:
  74:         a = atr(d["high"], d["low"], d["close"])
  75:         return [(p.kind, p.pivot_pos, p.confirm_pos, p.price) for p in zigzag(d["high"], d["low"], a, 1.5)]
  76: 
  77:     full = [p for p in pivots(df) if p[2] <= t]
  78:     assert pivots(df.iloc[: t + 1]) == full
  79:     assert [p for p in pivots(mutate_after(df, t)) if p[2] <= t] == full
  80: 
  81: 
  82: @settings(max_examples=80, deadline=None)
  83: @given(
  84:     st.lists(st.floats(-3.0, 3.0), min_size=5, max_size=150),
  85:     st.floats(0.5, 4.0),
  86: )
  87: def test_properties(steps: list[float], multiple: float) -> None:
  88:     close = 100 + np.cumsum(steps)
  89:     high, low = pd.Series(close + 0.5), pd.Series(close - 0.5)
  90:     a = pd.Series(np.ones(len(close)))
  91:     out = zigzag(high, low, a, multiple)
  92:     h, lo = high.to_numpy(), low.to_numpy()
  93:     for prev, cur in pairwise(out):
  94:         assert prev.kind != cur.kind  # strictly alternating
  95:         assert prev.confirm_pos < cur.confirm_pos
  96:         assert prev.pivot_pos < cur.pivot_pos <= cur.confirm_pos
  97:     for i, p in enumerate(out):
  98:         start = out[i - 1].pivot_pos + 1 if i else 0
  99:         window = slice(start, p.confirm_pos + 1)
 100:         if p.kind is SwingKind.HIGH:
 101:             assert p.price == h[window].max()  # the leg's extreme
 102:             assert p.price - lo[p.confirm_pos] >= multiple - 1e-9  # confirmed by a full reversal
 103:         else:
 104:             assert p.price == lo[window].min()
 105:             assert h[p.confirm_pos] - p.price >= multiple - 1e-9
 106:     # prefix consistency on arbitrary data
 107:     for t in (len(close) // 2, len(close) - 1):
 108:         cut = zigzag(high.iloc[: t + 1], low.iloc[: t + 1], a.iloc[: t + 1], multiple)
 109:         assert cut == [p for p in out if p.confirm_pos <= t]
