===== research/2026-10-26-three-bots/momentum.py =====
   1: """The owner's hypothesis, 2026-10-27, measured rather than argued.
   2: 
   3:     "D1 should not be that rigid. The order of hardness is H1, H4, D1 - meaning the more momentum D1
   4:     has, the more force that side has and the further it runs. Or not?"
   5: 
   6: Two claims live in that sentence, and they are measured separately:
   7: 
   8: A. **Direction is not a gate, it is a quantity.** A binary "the side must agree with the D1 regime" was
   9:    already tested and is nearly worthless: aligned -0.112 R against counter -0.173 R over 220 trades.
  10:    So the question is whether momentum *as a number* carries what the label does not.
  11: 
  12: B. **The further claim: more momentum on a side -> the trade on that side runs further.** The thing that
  13:    "runs further" is the favourable excursion, so the test is MFE in R against momentum - not the win
  14:    rate and not the final R, both of which the stop and the target cut off.
  15: 
  16: And the ordering H1 -> H4 -> D1: if the owner is right that D1 is the strongest, D1 momentum should
  17: separate the outcomes more than H4's, and H4's more than H1's. That ordering is a prediction, so it can
  18: fail: it is written here before the numbers are read.
  19: 
  20: Momentum of a frame, defined here (mine to define, the owner's idea): the close's distance from its own
  21: 50-bar mean, measured in that frame's own 14-bar Wilder ATR, signed - positive is up. Distance over a
  22: mean says where price is; dividing by that frame's ATR makes H1, H4 and D1 comparable, which a raw USD
  23: distance never is.
  24: 
  25:     .venv\\Scripts\\python research/2026-10-26-three-bots/momentum.py
  26: """
  27: 
  28: from __future__ import annotations
  29: 
  30: import sys
  31: from pathlib import Path
  32: 
  33: import numpy as np
  34: import pandas as pd
  35: 
  36: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  37: ROOT = HERE.parents[1]
  38: sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
  39: 
  40: from app.core.enums import Timeframe  # noqa: E402
  41: from app.engine.ai_trader import to_frame  # noqa: E402
  42: from app.market_data.history_store import ParquetHistoryStore  # noqa: E402
  43: 
  44: MEAN_BARS, ATR_BARS = 50, 14
  45: FRAMES = {"H1": 1, "H4": 4, "D1": 24}  # hours a bar covers
  46: 
  47: 
  48: def atr(frame: pd.DataFrame, n: int = ATR_BARS) -> pd.Series:
  49:     """Wilder's ATR, MetaTrader 5's own formula."""
  50:     prev = frame.close.shift(1)
  51:     tr = pd.concat([frame.high - frame.low, (frame.high - prev).abs(), (frame.low - prev).abs()], axis=1).max(
  52:         axis=1
  53:     )
  54:     return tr.ewm(alpha=1 / n, adjust=False, min_periods=n).mean()
  55: 
  56: 
  57: def momentum(h1: pd.DataFrame, hours: int) -> pd.DataFrame:
  58:     """One row per CLOSED bar of the frame: its momentum, and the time it became readable."""
  59:     opens = h1.copy()
  60:     opens.index = pd.DatetimeIndex(opens.index) - pd.Timedelta(hours=1)  # frames are close-stamped
  61:     rule = f"{hours}h" if hours < 24 else "1D"
  62:     f = opens.resample(rule).agg({"high": "max", "low": "min", "close": "last"}).dropna()
  63:     out = pd.DataFrame(index=f.index)
  64:     out["mom"] = (f.close - f.close.rolling(MEAN_BARS).mean()) / atr(f)
  65:     out["readable"] = f.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=hours)
  66:     return out.dropna()
  67: 
  68: 
  69: def read_at(table: pd.DataFrame, at: pd.Timestamp) -> float:
  70:     usable = table[table.readable <= at]
  71:     return float(usable.mom.iloc[-1]) if len(usable) else float("nan")
  72: 
  73: 
  74: def bins(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, name: str) -> None:
  75:     """Outcomes by how much momentum there was ON THE TRADE'S OWN SIDE."""
  76:     x = df.dropna(subset=[col])
  77:     if x.empty:
  78:         print(f"\n{name}: no data")
  79:         return
  80:     # signed momentum turned into "momentum behind this trade": +ve means the frame leans our way
  81:     behind = np.where(x.side == "BUY", x[col], -x[col])
  82:     x = x.assign(behind=behind)
  83:     edges = [-np.inf, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, np.inf]
  84:     labels = ["<-2", "-2..-1", "-1..0", "0..1", "1..2", ">2"]
  85:     x = x.assign(band=pd.cut(x.behind, edges, labels=labels))
  86:     print(f"\n### {name}\n")
  87:     print("| momentum behind the trade | trades | win | R a trade | **median MFE (R)** | fit / unseen R |")
  88:     print("|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
  89:     for lab in labels:
  90:         g = x[x.band == lab]
  91:         if len(g) < 8:
  92:             continue
  93:         fit, uns = g[g.fitted], g[~g.fitted]
  94:         both = f"{fit.r.mean():+.2f} / {uns.r.mean():+.2f}" if len(fit) and len(uns) else "-"
  95:         print(
  96:             f"| {lab} | {len(g)} | {(g.r > 0).mean():.0%} | {g.r.mean():+.3f} | "
  97:             f"**{g.mfe_r.median():.2f}** | {both} |"
  98:         )
  99:     c = x[["behind", "mfe_r"]].corr().iloc[0, 1]
 100:     cr = x[["behind", "r"]].corr().iloc[0, 1]
 101:     print(f"\ncorrelation with MFE **{c:+.3f}**, with the final R {cr:+.3f}  ({len(x)} trades)")
 102: 
 103: 
 104: def main() -> None:
 105:     sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
 106:     d = pd.read_csv(HERE / "edge_trades.csv")
 107:     d["at"] = pd.to_datetime(d["at"], utc=True)
 108:     hs = ParquetHistoryStore(ROOT / "data/history")
 109:     h1 = to_frame(hs.load("FBS-Demo", "XAUUSD", Timeframe.H1, None, None))
 110:     for name, hours in FRAMES.items():
 111:         table = momentum(h1, hours)
 112:         d[f"mom_{name}"] = [read_at(table, t) for t in d["at"]]
 113:     print("# The owner's momentum hypothesis (2026-10-27)\n")
 114:     print(
 115:         "Momentum = the close's distance from its own 50-bar mean in that frame's own 14-bar ATR, signed,\n"
 116:         "read from the last bar of the frame CLOSED before the entry. 'Behind the trade' flips the sign\n"
 117:         "for a sell, so a positive number always means the frame leans the way the trade is going.\n"
 118:     )
 119:     print(
 120:         f"{len(d)} trades, 2023-09 .. 2026-10. The prediction written first: D1 separates most, then H4, then H1."
 121:     )
 122:     for name in FRAMES:
 123:         bins(d, f"mom_{name}", f"{name} momentum behind the trade")
 124:     print("\n## The ordering, in one line each\n")
 125:     print("| frame | correlation with MFE | correlation with R |")
 126:     print("|---|---|---|")
 127:     for name in FRAMES:
 128:         x = d.dropna(subset=[f"mom_{name}"])
 129:         behind = np.where(x.side == "BUY", x[f"mom_{name}"], -x[f"mom_{name}"])
 130:         print(
 131:             f"| {name} | {np.corrcoef(behind, x.mfe_r)[0, 1]:+.3f} | {np.corrcoef(behind, x.r)[0, 1]:+.3f} |"
 132:         )
 133:     d.to_csv(HERE / "momentum_trades.csv", index=False)
 134: 
 135: 
 136: if __name__ == "__main__":
 137:     main()

===== research/2026-10-26-three-bots/bots.py =====
   1: """One trader, three bots (owner 2026-10-26: "the bots are like three traders, each trading its own setup at the
   2: same time, one account - at one decision point they cannot all enter: at most two, a trend one way and sideways";
   3: "make it profitable first - no before / after the exam").
   4: 
   5: Plain code, no AI, so it can be tuned fast. Each bot watches for its OWN structure at every M5 close inside the
   6: owner's windows (Asia 07:00-09:00, London 13:00-15:00 Thai, New York 08:00-11:30 NY) and holds its own position;
   7: a trend bot and the range bot can overlap, the two trend bots cannot (their conditions are opposites).
   8: 
   9: - DOWN: MetaTrader 5's ZigZag (12, 5, 3) on the H1 bars closed by then shows a lower high AND a lower low; its
  10:   last leg A (high) -> B (low); price pulled back RETRACE of it; an M5 box (6-24 bars, at most BOX of the leg
  11:   tall) - the quiet; an M5 close under the box = SELL; stop over the box by BUFFER of the box height;
  12:   take-profit at B, at least MIN_TP USD and MIN_R x the risk. UP: the mirror;
  13: - RANGE: the box comes from the BIG picture (owner 2026-10-26: "sideways has to be measured on H1-H4"):
  14:   ``range_tf`` names the frame whose ZigZag gives the box - H4, H1, or 'both' (the H4 box, and the H1 swings
  15:   must sit inside it). That frame must not be stepping (no lower-high-and-lower-low, no higher pair), and price
  16:   must be inside the box. Then an M5 bar over the box top closing back under it = SELL (a sweep closed back),
  17:   stop over that bar's high + BUFFER x its range, take-profit TP_RANGE of the way to the box low; the mirror at
  18:   the bottom. ``range_mode`` 'opposite' takes the other side, 'break' trades an M5 close out of the box;
  19: - shared by the account: no entry into a push still running toward the stop (the 60 minutes before ran more than
  20:   RUN x the stop distance), and the windows;
  21: - the exit, ``exit_mode``: 'tp' the stop and take-profit alone; 'be' the stop to break-even once the trade is
  22:   BE_AT x risk ahead; 'trail' the same, then the stop follows the extreme of the last TRAIL_MIN minutes of M1
  23:   bars; 'struct' out when STRUCT_BARS M5 bars close back across the entry. 'tp' leans on where the test stops
  24:   (the Thai day's end carried 29-48 % of each bot's gross R), so it is a baseline, not a design;
  25: - SL / TP on the M1 bars (bid), the spread SPREAD USD off every trade; a trade still open at the Thai day's end
  26:   is closed there.
  27: 
  28:     .venv/Scripts/python research/2026-10-26-three-bots/bots.py
  29: """
  30: 
  31: from __future__ import annotations
  32: 
  33: import sys
  34: from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace
  35: from pathlib import Path
  36: 
  37: import pandas as pd
  38: 
  39: ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
  40: sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
  41: 
  42: from app.core.enums import Timeframe  # noqa: E402
  43: from app.engine.ai_trader import to_frame  # noqa: E402
  44: from app.market_data.history_store import ParquetHistoryStore  # noqa: E402
  45: from app.strategy.zigzag import zigzag  # noqa: E402
  46: 
  47: TH, NY = "Asia/Bangkok", "America/New_York"
  48: START, END = pd.Timestamp("2025-10-20", tz="UTC"), pd.Timestamp("2026-10-24", tz="UTC")
  49: 
  50: 
  51: @dataclass
  52: class P:
  53:     retrace: tuple[float, float] = (0.382, 0.786)
  54:     box: float = 0.25  # the box at most this share of the H1 leg tall
  55:     buffer: float = 0.2  # the stop past the box by this share of the box height
  56:     min_tp: float = 0.0  # the 5 USD floor, off since 2026-10-27 (it rejected 1 of 496 signals)
  57:     min_r: float = 1.0
  58:     tp_range: float = 0.5
  59:     run: float = 1.2
  60:     spread: float = 0.30
  61:     exit_mode: str = "tp"  # tp | be | trail | struct (see the module docstring)
  62:     be_at: float = 1.0  # the trade must be this many R ahead before the stop moves
  63:     trail_min: int = 30  # 'trail': the stop follows the extreme of the last this many minutes of M1 bars
  64:     struct_bars: int = 3  # 'struct': this many M5 closes back across the entry end the trade
  65:     max_hours: float = (
  66:         0  # the trade is closed at this bar's close after this many hours (0: at the day's end)
  67:     )
  68:     bots: tuple[str, ...] = ("DOWN", "UP", "RANGE")
  69:     d1: bool = False  # the trend bots only with the D1 regime (the close against its 50-day mean)
  70:     range_mode: str = "fade"  # fade: sell a sweep closed back; opposite: the same trigger, the other side;
  71:     # break: an M5 close out of the box, traded with the break
  72:     range_tf: str = "H4"  # where the box comes from: "H4", "H1" or "both" (the H4 box with H1 inside it)
  73:     range_flat: bool = True  # the box frame must not be stepping up or down
  74: 
  75: 
  76: @dataclass
  77: class Trade:
  78:     bot: str
  79:     side: str
  80:     at: pd.Timestamp
  81:     entry: float
  82:     stop: float
  83:     tp: float
  84:     r: float = 0.0
  85:     out: str = ""
  86:     extra: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
  87: 
  88: 
  89: # The configuration the app actually ships (app/strategy/trend_box.py, range_break.py and the three_bots
  90: # lane of config.yaml). It is a dict because the three bots do NOT share one P: RANGE differs from the
  91: # others in KIND - "break" is an M5 close out of the box, while P()'s default "fade" sells a sweep that
  92: # closed back inside, a different setup entirely.
  93: #
  94: # On 2026-10-27 a research script measured P() instead of this and produced an afternoon of conclusions
  95: # about a setup we do not run (the shipped bots are +0.102 R a trade; the defaults are -0.106). `run`
  96: # now refuses the bare defaults so that cannot happen silently again.
  97: SHIPPED: dict[str, "P"] = {}  # filled below, once P exists
  98: 
  99: 
 100: def _shipped() -> dict[str, "P"]:
 101:     return {
 102:         "DOWN": replace(P(), bots=("DOWN",), box=0.2, buffer=0.2, min_r=1.2),
 103:         "UP": replace(P(), bots=("UP",), box=0.33, buffer=0.2, min_r=1.2),
 104:         "RANGE": replace(
 105:             P(), bots=("RANGE",), range_tf="H4", range_mode="break", tp_range=0.5, buffer=0.3, min_r=0.8
 106:         ),  # fmt: skip
 107:     }
 108: 
 109: 
 110: SHIPPED.update(_shipped())
 111: 
 112: 
 113: def windows(day: pd.Timestamp) -> list[tuple[pd.Timestamp, pd.Timestamp]]:
 114:     th, ny = pd.Timestamp(day.date(), tz=TH), pd.Timestamp(day.date(), tz=NY)
 115:     return [
 116:         ((th + pd.Timedelta(hours=7)).tz_convert("UTC"), (th + pd.Timedelta(hours=9)).tz_convert("UTC")),
 117:         ((th + pd.Timedelta(hours=13)).tz_convert("UTC"), (th + pd.Timedelta(hours=15)).tz_convert("UTC")),
 118:         (
 119:             (ny + pd.Timedelta(hours=8)).tz_convert("UTC"),
 120:             (ny + pd.Timedelta(hours=11, minutes=30)).tz_convert("UTC"),
 121:         ),
 122:     ]
 123: 
 124: 
 125: def market(h1: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[str, list]:  # the census only: the bots no longer switch on it
 126:     sw, _ = zigzag(h1.high.to_numpy(float), h1.low.to_numpy(float))
 127:     highs = [s for s in sw if s.kind == 1]
 128:     lows = [s for s in sw if s.kind == -1]
 129:     if len(highs) < 2 or len(lows) < 2:
 130:         return "NONE", sw
 131:     if highs[-1].price < highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price < lows[-2].price:
 132:         return "DOWN", sw
 133:     if highs[-1].price > highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price > lows[-2].price:
 134:         return "UP", sw
 135:     return "RANGE", sw
 136: 
 137: 
 138: def settle(m1: pd.DataFrame, t: Trade, until: pd.Timestamp, p: P) -> Trade:
 139:     """Walk the M1 bars from the entry to ``until``: the stop (moved by ``exit_mode``) and the take-profit, the
 140:     worse of the two first when a bar holds both; else the last close. The spread is taken off once."""
 141:     if p.max_hours:
 142:         until = min(until, t.at + pd.Timedelta(hours=p.max_hours))
 143:     x = m1[(m1.index >= t.at) & (m1.index < until)]
 144:     sell = t.side == "SELL"
 145:     risk = abs(t.stop - t.entry)
 146:     hi = x.high.to_numpy()
 147:     lo = x.low.to_numpy()
 148:     cl = x.close.to_numpy()
 149:     ix = x.index
 150:     stop, best = t.stop, t.entry  # best: the furthest price reached in the trade's favour
 151:     exit_px, out, when = None, ("time" if p.max_hours else "eod"), until
 152:     back = 0  # 'struct': M5 closes in a row back across the entry
 153:     for i in range(len(x)):
 154:         if (hi[i] >= stop) if sell else (lo[i] <= stop):
 155:             exit_px, out, when = stop, ("SL" if stop == t.stop else "trail"), ix[i]
 156:             break
 157:         if (lo[i] <= t.tp) if sell else (hi[i] >= t.tp):
 158:             exit_px, out, when = t.tp, "TP", ix[i]
 159:             break
 160:         best = min(best, lo[i]) if sell else max(best, hi[i])
 161:         ahead = (t.entry - best) if sell else (best - t.entry)
 162:         if p.exit_mode in ("be", "trail") and ahead >= p.be_at * risk:
 163:             moved = t.entry
 164:             if p.exit_mode == "trail" and i >= 1:
 165:                 j = max(0, i - p.trail_min + 1)
 166:                 moved = float(hi[j : i + 1].max()) if sell else float(lo[j : i + 1].min())
 167:             stop = min(stop, max(moved, t.entry)) if sell else max(stop, min(moved, t.entry))
 168:         if p.exit_mode == "struct" and (i + 1) % 5 == 0:
 169:             back = back + 1 if ((cl[i] > t.entry) if sell else (cl[i] < t.entry)) else 0
 170:             if back >= p.struct_bars:
 171:                 exit_px, out, when = float(cl[i]), "struct", ix[i]
 172:                 break
 173:     if exit_px is None:
 174:         exit_px = float(cl[-1]) if len(x) else t.entry
 175:     gain = (t.entry - exit_px) if sell else (exit_px - t.entry)
 176:     t.r, t.out = (gain - p.spread) / risk, out
 177:     t.extra["closed"] = when
 178:     return t
 179: 
 180: 
 181: def run(
 182:     f: dict, params: P | dict[str, P], start: pd.Timestamp = START, end: pd.Timestamp = END
 183: ) -> list[Trade]:
 184:     """Every bot of ``params`` (one P for all its bots, or one P per bot) side by side, each with its own
 185:     position, so a trend trade and a range trade can be open at once."""
 186:     if isinstance(params, P) and params == P():
 187:         raise ValueError(
 188:             "run() was given the bare P() defaults. Those are not the configuration we ship - RANGE's "
 189:             "default range_mode is 'fade', while the shipped bot is 'break'. Pass bots.SHIPPED (a dict, "
 190:             "one P per bot), or replace(P(), ...) when you mean to vary something deliberately. "
 191:             "(The trap that cost 2026-10-27's afternoon.)"
 192:         )
 193:     per: dict[str, P] = params if isinstance(params, dict) else {b: params for b in params.bots}
 194:     m1, h1 = f[Timeframe.M1], f[Timeframe.H1]
 195:     h4 = h1.resample("4h").agg({"open": "first", "high": "max", "low": "min", "close": "last"}).dropna()
 196:     m1 = m1[(m1.index >= start - pd.Timedelta(days=3)) & (m1.index < end + pd.Timedelta(days=2))]
 197:     m5 = m1.resample("5min").agg({"open": "first", "high": "max", "low": "min", "close": "last"}).dropna()
 198:     trades: list[Trade] = []
 199:     busy = dict.fromkeys(per, start)  # each bot's own position: free again from this time
 200:     for day in pd.date_range(start, end, freq="D"):
 201:         if day.weekday() >= 5:
 202:             continue
 203:         day_end = (pd.Timestamp(day.date(), tz=TH) + pd.Timedelta(days=1, hours=7)).tz_convert("UTC")
 204:         for w0, w1 in windows(day):
 205:             bars = m5[(m5.index >= w0 - pd.Timedelta(hours=2)) & (m5.index < w1)]
 206:             seen, sw, sw4 = None, [], []
 207:             for i in range(len(bars)):
 208:                 t_close = bars.index[i] + pd.Timedelta(minutes=5)
 209:                 if t_close <= w0 or t_close > w1:
 210:                     continue
 211:                 hx = h1[h1.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=1) <= t_close].iloc[-400:]
 212:                 if seen != len(hx):
 213:                     seen = len(hx)
 214:                     sw, _ = zigzag(hx.high.to_numpy(float), hx.low.to_numpy(float))
 215:                     h4x = h4[h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4) <= t_close].iloc[-300:]
 216:                     sw4, _ = zigzag(h4x.high.to_numpy(float), h4x.low.to_numpy(float))
 217:                 if len(sw) < 3:
 218:                     continue
 219:                 past = bars.iloc[: i + 1]
 220:                 for bot, p in per.items():
 221:                     if t_close < busy[bot]:
 222:                         continue  # this bot's own trade is still open
 223:                     tr = signal(bot, sw, sw4, past, p, t_close)
 224:                     if tr is None:
 225:                         continue
 226:                     before = m1[(m1.index >= t_close - pd.Timedelta(minutes=60)) & (m1.index < t_close)]
 227:                     runup = (
 228:                         (tr.entry - before.low.min()) if tr.side == "SELL" else (before.high.max() - tr.entry)
 229:                     )
 230:                     if p.run and runup > p.run * abs(tr.stop - tr.entry):
 231:                         continue
 232:                     tr = settle(m1, tr, day_end, p)
 233:                     trades.append(tr)
 234:                     busy[bot] = tr.extra["closed"]
 235:     return sorted(trades, key=lambda x: x.at)
 236: 
 237: 
 238: def stepping(sws: list) -> str:
 239:     """'DOWN' (lower high and lower low), 'UP', or 'FLAT' - from the last two confirmed swings of each kind."""
 240:     highs = [s for s in sws if s.kind == 1]
 241:     lows = [s for s in sws if s.kind == -1]
 242:     if len(highs) < 2 or len(lows) < 2:
 243:         return "FLAT"
 244:     if highs[-1].price < highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price < lows[-2].price:
 245:         return "DOWN"
 246:     if highs[-1].price > highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price > lows[-2].price:
 247:         return "UP"
 248:     return "FLAT"
 249: 
 250: 
 251: def range_box(sw: list, sw4: list, p: P) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
 252:     """(top, bottom) of the big-picture box, or None when that frame is not sideways."""
 253:     big = sw4 if p.range_tf in ("H4", "both") else sw
 254:     if len(big) < 2:
 255:         return None
 256:     if p.range_flat and stepping(big) != "FLAT":
 257:         return None
 258:     highs = [s.price for s in big if s.kind == 1]
 259:     lows = [s.price for s in big if s.kind == -1]
 260:     if not highs or not lows:
 261:         return None
 262:     top, floor = highs[-1], lows[-1]
 263:     if top <= floor:
 264:         return None
 265:     if p.range_tf == "both":  # the H1 swings have to sit inside the H4 box
 266:         h1h = [s.price for s in sw if s.kind == 1][-1:]
 267:         h1l = [s.price for s in sw if s.kind == -1][-1:]
 268:         if not h1h or not h1l or h1h[0] > top or h1l[0] < floor:
 269:             return None
 270:     return top, floor
 271: 
 272: 
 273: def signal(bot: str, sw: list, sw4: list, past: pd.DataFrame, p: P, t: pd.Timestamp) -> Trade | None:
 274:     """The bot's own setup on these bars, or None. Each bot tests its own structure, so a down leg and a range
 275:     can hold at the same moment; the two trend bots cannot, their conditions being opposites."""
 276:     price = float(past.close.iloc[-1])
 277:     highs = [s for s in sw if s.kind == 1]
 278:     lows = [s for s in sw if s.kind == -1]
 279:     if len(highs) < 2 or len(lows) < 2:
 280:         return None
 281:     if bot in ("DOWN", "UP"):
 282:         down = bot == "DOWN"
 283:         stepping = (
 284:             (highs[-1].price < highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price < lows[-2].price)
 285:             if down
 286:             else (highs[-1].price > highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price > lows[-2].price)
 287:         )
 288:         if not stepping:
 289:             return None
 290:         a, b = sw[-2], sw[-1]  # the last confirmed leg
 291:         if (a.kind, b.kind) != ((1, -1) if down else (-1, 1)):
 292:             return None
 293:         leg = abs(a.price - b.price)
 294:         if leg <= 0:
 295:             return None
 296:         for n in range(24, 5, -1):  # the longest box that still fits
 297:             if len(past) < n + 1:
 298:                 continue
 299:             box = past.iloc[-n - 1 : -1]
 300:             top, floor = float(box.high.max()), float(box.low.min())
 301:             if top - floor > p.box * leg:
 302:                 continue
 303:             mid = (top + floor) / 2
 304:             retr = (mid - b.price) / leg if down else (b.price - mid) / leg
 305:             if not (p.retrace[0] <= retr <= p.retrace[1]):
 306:                 return None
 307:             if down and price < floor:
 308:                 stop, tp = top + p.buffer * (top - floor), b.price
 309:             elif not down and price > top:
 310:                 stop, tp = floor - p.buffer * (top - floor), b.price
 311:             else:
 312:                 return None
 313:             risk, reward = abs(stop - price), abs(price - tp)
 314:             if reward < p.min_tp or reward < p.min_r * risk or (tp >= price if down else tp <= price):
 315:                 return None
 316:             side = "SELL" if down else "BUY"
 317:             return Trade(bot, side, t, price, stop, tp, extra={"retrace": round(retr, 2), "box": n})
 318:         return None
 319:     box = range_box(sw, sw4, p)
 320:     if box is None:
 321:         return None
 322:     hh, ll = box
 323:     bar = past.iloc[-1]
 324:     rng = hh - ll
 325:     if p.range_mode == "break":  # price leaves the box: the close through decides, no 'inside' gate
 326:         prev = float(past.close.iloc[-2]) if len(past) > 1 else price
 327:         if price > hh >= prev:
 328:             stop, tp, side = hh - p.buffer * rng * 0.25, price + p.tp_range * rng, "BUY"
 329:         elif price < ll <= prev:
 330:             stop, tp, side = ll + p.buffer * rng * 0.25, price - p.tp_range * rng, "SELL"
 331:         else:
 332:             return None
 333:         risk, reward = abs(stop - price), abs(tp - price)
 334:         if risk <= 0 or reward < p.min_tp or reward < p.min_r * risk:
 335:             return None
 336:         return Trade("RANGE", side, t, price, stop, tp, extra={"range": round(rng, 1)})
 337:     if not ll < price < hh:  # a fade is taken from inside the box only
 338:         return None
 339:     if bar.high > hh and bar.close < hh:
 340:         stop = float(bar.high) + p.buffer * float(bar.high - bar.low)
 341:         tp = price - p.tp_range * (price - ll)
 342:         side = "SELL"
 343:     elif bar.low < ll and bar.close > ll:
 344:         stop = float(bar.low) - p.buffer * float(bar.high - bar.low)
 345:         tp = price + p.tp_range * (hh - price)
 346:         side = "BUY"
 347:     else:
 348:         return None
 349:     if p.range_mode == "opposite":  # the same moment, the other side: stop and take-profit mirrored
 350:         side = "BUY" if side == "SELL" else "SELL"
 351:         stop, tp = 2 * price - stop, 2 * price - tp
 352:     risk, reward = abs(stop - price), abs(tp - price)
 353:     if reward < p.min_tp or reward < p.min_r * risk:
 354:         return None
 355:     return Trade("RANGE", side, t, price, stop, tp, extra={"range": round(rng, 1)})
 356: 
 357: 
 358: def report(trades: list[Trade], title: str) -> None:
 359:     df = pd.DataFrame([{"bot": t.bot, "side": t.side, "at": t.at, "r": t.r, "out": t.out} for t in trades])
 360:     print(f"\n== {title}: {len(df)} trades ==")
 361:     if not len(df):
 362:         return
 363:     print("| bot | trades | win % | R / trade (after spread) | total R | weeks + / - | worst streak |")
 364:     print("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
 365:     for bot, g in [*df.groupby("bot"), ("ALL", df)]:
 366:         wk = g.groupby(g["at"].dt.tz_convert(TH).dt.to_period("W")).r.sum()
 367:         streak = cur = 0
 368:         for r in g.r:
 369:             cur = cur + 1 if r < 0 else 0
 370:             streak = max(streak, cur)
 371:         print(
 372:             f"| {bot} | {len(g)} | {(g.r > 0).mean():.0%} | {g.r.mean():+.3f} | {g.r.sum():+.1f} | "
 373:             f"{(wk > 0).sum()} / {(wk <= 0).sum()} | {streak} |"
 374:         )
 375: 
 376: 
 377: def main() -> None:
 378:     sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
 379:     hs = ParquetHistoryStore(ROOT / "data/history")
 380:     f = {t: to_frame(hs.load("FBS-Demo", "XAUUSD", t, None, None)) for t in (Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.H1)}
 381:     report(run(f, SHIPPED), "the three bots as shipped, 2025-10-20 .. 2026-10-23")
 382: 
 383: 
 384: if __name__ == "__main__":
 385:     main()

===== research/2026-10-26-three-bots/audit.py =====
   1: """Where each bot's profit really comes from, before believing the totals (memory: cross-check every number).
   2: 
   3: Per bot: the R split by how the trade ended (take-profit, stop, or closed at the Thai day's end), and how long
   4: trades are held - a scalp system that earns most of its R from positions carried to the next morning is not the
   5: system the owner asked for.
   6: 
   7:     .venv/Scripts/python research/2026-10-26-three-bots/audit.py
   8: """
   9: 
  10: from __future__ import annotations
  11: 
  12: import sys
  13: from dataclasses import replace
  14: from pathlib import Path
  15: 
  16: import pandas as pd
  17: 
  18: HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  19: sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
  20: sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE.parents[1]))
  21: 
  22: import bots  # noqa: E402
  23: 
  24: from app.core.enums import Timeframe  # noqa: E402
  25: from app.engine.ai_trader import to_frame  # noqa: E402
  26: from app.market_data.history_store import ParquetHistoryStore  # noqa: E402
  27: 
  28: # The one definition lives in bots.SHIPPED, next to P, so the two can never drift apart
  29: # (2026-10-27: measuring P() instead of this produced an afternoon of wrong conclusions).
  30: BEST = bots.SHIPPED
  31: 
  32: 
  33: def load() -> dict:
  34:     hs = ParquetHistoryStore(HERE.parents[1] / "data/history")
  35:     f = {}
  36:     for tf in (Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.H1):
  37:         x = to_frame(hs.load("FBS-Demo", "XAUUSD", tf, None, None))
  38:         f[tf] = x[
  39:             (x.index >= bots.START - pd.Timedelta(days=60)) & (x.index < bots.END + pd.Timedelta(days=3))
  40:         ]
  41:     return f
  42: 
  43: 
  44: def main() -> None:
  45:     sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
  46:     f = load()
  47:     rows = []
  48:     for name, p in BEST.items():
  49:         for t in bots.run(f, p):
  50:             rows.append({"bot": name, "out": t.out, "r": t.r, "at": t.at, "closed": t.extra["closed"]})
  51:     d = pd.DataFrame(rows)
  52:     d["hours"] = (pd.to_datetime(d["closed"]) - pd.to_datetime(d["at"])).dt.total_seconds() / 3600
  53: 
  54:     print("== where each bot's R comes from ==")
  55:     print("| bot | how it ended | trades | R | share of that bot's R |")
  56:     print("|---|---|---|---|---|")
  57:     for bot, g in d.groupby("bot"):
  58:         tot = g.r.sum()
  59:         for out, h in g.groupby("out"):
  60:             share = f"{h.r.sum() / tot * 100:+.0f}%" if tot else "-"
  61:             print(f"| {bot} | {out} | {len(h)} | {h.r.sum():+.1f} | {share} |")
  62: 
  63:     print("\n== how long a trade is held ==")
  64:     for bot, g in d.groupby("bot"):
  65:         print(f"- {bot}: median {g.hours.median():.1f} h, longest {g.hours.max():.1f} h; "
  66:               f"held over 4 h: {int((g.hours > 4).sum())} of {len(g)}")  # fmt: skip
  67: 
  68:     print("\n== with a time stop: the trade closed this long after it opened, at that bar's close ==")
  69:     print("| time stop | trades | win % | R / trade | total R |")
  70:     print("|---|---|---|---|---|")
  71:     m1 = f[Timeframe.M1]
  72:     for hours in (1, 2, 4, 8, None):
  73:         out = []
  74:         for name, p in BEST.items():
  75:             for t in bots.run(f, p):
  76:                 limit = t.at + pd.Timedelta(hours=hours) if hours else t.extra["closed"]
  77:                 x = m1[(m1.index >= t.at) & (m1.index < min(limit, t.extra["closed"]))]
  78:                 sell = t.side == "SELL"
  79:                 risk = abs(t.stop - t.entry)
  80:                 px = None
  81:                 for _, b in x.iterrows():
  82:                     if (b.high >= t.stop) if sell else (b.low <= t.stop):
  83:                         px = t.stop
  84:                         break
  85:                     if (b.low <= t.tp) if sell else (b.high >= t.tp):
  86:                         px = t.tp
  87:                         break
  88:                 if px is None:
  89:                     px = float(x.close.iloc[-1]) if len(x) else t.entry
  90:                 gain = (t.entry - px) if sell else (px - t.entry)
  91:                 out.append((gain - p.spread) / risk)
  92:         s = pd.Series(out)
  93:         label = f"{hours} h" if hours else "none (to the day's end)"
  94:         print(f"| {label} | {len(s)} | {(s > 0).mean():.0%} | {s.mean():+.3f} | {s.sum():+.1f} |")
  95: 
  96: 
  97: if __name__ == "__main__":
  98:     main()

===== app/engine/backends.py =====
   1: """Execution backends of the engine (TAA-1206): where accepted decisions go and whose book is managed.
   2: 
   3: - :class:`PaperBackend` (PAPER): simulated fills on live quotes, the paper book in the local database, the
   4:   paper position manager. Real account positions only count toward exposure.
   5: - :class:`DemoBackend` (DEMO, Phase 12): real broker orders on the **demo** account through the
   6:   :class:`OrderManager`, the :class:`Reconciler` on every maintenance pass, and the
   7:   :class:`BrokerPositionManager`; the :class:`BrokerTradeBook` books closed positions. A kill switch in
   8:   FLATTEN mode closes every bot position (when allowed).
   9: 
  10: The engine talks to either through the same small interface, so the decision path is identical in both.
  11: """
  12: 
  13: from __future__ import annotations
  14: 
  15: import logging
  16: from collections.abc import Collection
  17: from datetime import datetime, timedelta
  18: from typing import Protocol
  19: 
  20: from app.broker.gateway import MarketDataGateway
  21: from app.broker.models import BrokerOrder, BrokerPosition, Deal
  22: from app.core.clock import Clock
  23: from app.core.enums import ExitReason
  24: from app.core.errors import TaaError
  25: from app.engine.broker_positions import BrokerPositionManager
  26: from app.engine.broker_trades import BrokerTradeBook
  27: from app.engine.decision_engine import DecisionRecord
  28: from app.engine.order_manager import IntentState, OrderManager
  29: from app.engine.paper import PaperExecution
  30: from app.engine.plan_supervisor import PlanSupervisor
  31: from app.engine.position_manager import PositionManager
  32: from app.engine.reconciler import Reconciler
  33: from app.market_data.data_models import SymbolSpec
  34: from app.risk.kill_switch import KillMode, KillSwitch
  35: from app.risk.position_sizer import AccountFunds
  36: from app.strategy.signal_models import StrategyContext
  37: 
  38: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  39: PLACED_STATES = frozenset(
  40:     {
  41:         IntentState.FILLED,
  42:         IntentState.PARTIAL,
  43:         IntentState.PROTECTED,
  44:         IntentState.RECONCILED,
  45:         IntentState.UNKNOWN,
  46:         IntentState.PLACED,
  47:     }
  48: )
  49: 
  50: 
  51: class Backend(Protocol):
  52:     name: str
  53: 
  54:     def funds(self) -> AccountFunds: ...
  55: 
  56:     def book(self) -> list[BrokerPosition]: ...
  57: 
  58:     def pending_orders(self) -> list[BrokerOrder]:
  59:         """Resting orders whose risk counts toward portfolio heat (TAA-1207)."""
  60:         ...
  61: 
  62:     def position_groups(self) -> dict[int, str]:
  63:         """Position ticket -> entry plan: the parts of one plan count as one position (TAA-1207)."""
  64:         ...
  65: 
  66:     def equity(self) -> float: ...
  67: 
  68:     def new_deals(self) -> list[Deal]: ...
  69: 
  70:     def on_quote(self, symbol: str, bid: float, ask: float, atr: float | None, now: datetime) -> None: ...
  71: 
  72:     def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None: ...
  73: 
  74:     def on_lane_bar(
  75:         self,
  76:         symbol: str,
  77:         ctx: StrategyContext,
  78:         strategies: Collection[str],
  79:         close_check: bool = False,
  80:     ) -> None:
  81:         """A lane's closed entry bar: its strategies' own position management (``manage_position``),
  82:         and with ``close_check`` their ``should_close`` as well - an extra lane's bars never reach
  83:         :meth:`on_bar`, which is where the main lane asks that question."""
  84:         ...
  85: 
  86:     def place(self, record: DecisionRecord, spec: SymbolSpec, magic: int) -> bool: ...
  87: 
  88:     def maintain(self) -> None: ...
  89: 
  90:     def open_positions(self) -> int: ...
  91: 
  92:     def close_position(self, ticket: int, reason: ExitReason) -> str:
  93:         """Close one bot position (remote POSITION_CLOSE); raises TaaError when it is unknown or fails."""
  94:         ...
  95: 
  96: 
  97: class PaperBackend:
  98:     name = "paper"
  99: 
 100:     def __init__(
 101:         self,
 102:         paper: PaperExecution,
 103:         positions: PositionManager,
 104:         market: MarketDataGateway,
 105:         kill_switch: KillSwitch | None = None,
 106:     ) -> None:
 107:         self.paper = paper
 108:         self.positions = positions
 109:         self.market = market
 110:         self.kill_switch = kill_switch
 111:         self._booked = 0
 112:         self._flattened = False
 113: 
 114:     def funds(self) -> AccountFunds:
 115:         return self.paper.broker.funds()
 116: 
 117:     def book(self) -> list[BrokerPosition]:
 118:         """Paper positions plus every real position on the account (manual trades count toward exposure)."""
 119:         try:
 120:             real = self.market.positions()
 121:         except TaaError:
 122:             log.warning("could not read account positions; exposure uses the paper book only")
 123:             real = []
 124:         return [*self.paper.broker.broker_positions(), *real]
 125: 
 126:     def pending_orders(self) -> list[BrokerOrder]:
 127:         """Paper limit parts plus the account's own resting orders (manual ones count under ``count``)."""
 128:         try:
 129:             real = self.market.orders()
 130:         except TaaError:
 131:             log.warning("could not read account orders; heat uses the paper orders only")
 132:             real = []
 133:         return [*self.paper.pending_orders(), *real]
 134: 
 135:     def position_groups(self) -> dict[int, str]:
 136:         return self.paper.plan_groups()
 137: 
 138:     def equity(self) -> float:
 139:         return self.paper.broker.equity
 140: 
 141:     def new_deals(self) -> list[Deal]:
 142:         new = self.paper.broker.deals[self._booked :]
 143:         self._booked = len(self.paper.broker.deals)
 144:         return new
 145: 
 146:     def on_quote(self, symbol: str, bid: float, ask: float, atr: float | None, now: datetime) -> None:
 147:         self.paper.on_quote(symbol, bid, ask, now)
 148:         self.positions.on_quote(symbol, bid, ask, atr)
 149: 
 150:     def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
 151:         self.positions.on_bar(symbol, ctx)
 152: 
 153:     def on_lane_bar(
 154:         self,
 155:         symbol: str,
 156:         ctx: StrategyContext,
 157:         strategies: Collection[str],
 158:         close_check: bool = False,
 159:     ) -> None:
 160:         self.positions.on_lane_bar(symbol, ctx, strategies, close_check)
 161: 
 162:     def place(self, record: DecisionRecord, spec: SymbolSpec, magic: int) -> bool:
 163:         return any(not p.duplicate for p in self.paper.place(record, magic))
 164: 
 165:     def maintain(self) -> None:
 166:         self.paper.save_marks()
 167:         state = None if self.kill_switch is None else self.kill_switch.state()
 168:         self.paper.supervise_plans(state is not None and state.active)
 169:         if state is not None and state.active and state.mode is KillMode.FLATTEN and not self._flattened:
 170:             for ticket in list(self.paper.broker.positions):  # paper positions close at the next quote
 171:                 self.paper.request_close(ticket, ExitReason.KILL_SWITCH)
 172:             self._flattened = True
 173:         elif state is None or not state.active:
 174:             self._flattened = False
 175: 
 176:     def open_positions(self) -> int:
 177:         return len(self.paper.broker.positions)
 178: 
 179:     def close_position(self, ticket: int, reason: ExitReason) -> str:
 180:         if ticket not in self.paper.broker.positions:
 181:             raise TaaError(f"no open paper position #{ticket}")
 182:         self.paper.request_close(ticket, reason)
 183:         return f"paper position #{ticket} closes at the next quote"
 184: 
 185: 
 186: class DemoBackend:
 187:     name = "demo"
 188: 
 189:     def __init__(
 190:         self,
 191:         orders: OrderManager,
 192:         reconciler: Reconciler,
 193:         positions: BrokerPositionManager,
 194:         market: MarketDataGateway,
 195:         kill_switch: KillSwitch,
 196:         clock: Clock,
 197:         *,
 198:         plans: PlanSupervisor | None = None,
 199:         trades: BrokerTradeBook | None = None,
 200:     ) -> None:
 201:         self.orders = orders
 202:         self.reconciler = reconciler
 203:         self.plans = plans
 204:         self.trades = trades
 205:         self.positions = positions
 206:         self.market = market
 207:         self.kill_switch = kill_switch
 208:         self.clock = clock
 209:         self._flattened = False
 210: 
 211:     def funds(self) -> AccountFunds:
 212:         a = self.market.account()
 213:         return AccountFunds(equity=a.equity, balance=a.balance, margin=a.margin, margin_free=a.margin_free)
 214: 
 215:     def book(self) -> list[BrokerPosition]:
 216:         return self.market.positions()
 217: 
 218:     def pending_orders(self) -> list[BrokerOrder]:
 219:         return self.market.orders()
 220: 
 221:     def position_groups(self) -> dict[int, str]:
 222:         return self.orders.plan_groups()
 223: 
 224:     def equity(self) -> float:
 225:         return self.market.account().equity
 226: 
 227:     def new_deals(self) -> list[Deal]:
 228:         """Recent account deals; the loss tracker books each ticket once."""
 229:         now = self.clock.now_utc()
 230:         try:
 231:             return self.market.deals(now - timedelta(days=8), now + timedelta(days=1))
 232:         except TaaError:
 233:             log.warning("history_deals_get failed; loss tracking uses equity only this time")
 234:             return []
 235: 
 236:     def on_quote(self, symbol: str, bid: float, ask: float, atr: float | None, now: datetime) -> None:
 237:         self.positions.on_quote(symbol, bid, ask, atr)
 238: 
 239:     def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
 240:         self.positions.on_bar(symbol, ctx)
 241: 
 242:     def on_lane_bar(
 243:         self,
 244:         symbol: str,
 245:         ctx: StrategyContext,
 246:         strategies: Collection[str],
 247:         close_check: bool = False,
 248:     ) -> None:
 249:         self.positions.on_lane_bar(symbol, ctx, strategies, close_check)
 250: 
 251:     def place(self, record: DecisionRecord, spec: SymbolSpec, magic: int) -> bool:
 252:         outcomes = self.orders.execute(record, spec, magic)
 253:         return any(o.state in PLACED_STATES for o in outcomes)
 254: 
 255:     def maintain(self) -> None:
 256:         self.reconciler.run()
 257:         if self.plans is not None:
 258:             self.plans.run()  # resting limit parts: fills, lifetime, kill switch, closed market parts
 259:         if self.trades is not None:
 260:             self.trades.run()  # closed positions into the trade history (TAA-1208)
 261:         state = self.kill_switch.state()
 262:         if state.active and state.mode is KillMode.FLATTEN and not self._flattened:
 263:             self.positions.flatten(state.reason or "kill switch FLATTEN")
 264:             self._flattened = True
 265:         elif not state.active:
 266:             self._flattened = False
 267: 
 268:     def open_positions(self) -> int:
 269:         try:
 270:             return len(self.positions.bot_positions())
 271:         except TaaError:
 272:             return 0
 273: 
 274:     def close_position(self, ticket: int, reason: ExitReason) -> str:
 275:         """Only the bot's own positions (its magic range): a remote command never touches manual trades."""
 276:         pos = next((p for p in self.positions.bot_positions() if p.ticket == ticket), None)
 277:         if pos is None:
 278:             raise TaaError(f"no open bot position #{ticket}")
 279:         result = self.positions.close(pos, self.market.symbol_spec(pos.symbol), reason)
 280:         if not result.ok:
 281:             raise TaaError(f"close of #{ticket} failed: retcode {result.retcode}")
 282:         return f"position #{ticket} closed"

===== app/engine/position_manager.py =====
   1: """Position manager for PAPER positions (PLAN Â§A11; TAA-603). Milestone 2 reuses it for broker positions.
   2: 
   3: Every quote (1â€“2 s): the shared rules of :mod:`app.execution.management` (break-even, ATR trailing, time
   4: stop). Every new closed entry bar: the bar count for the time stop, and the strategy's own close signal
   5: (:meth:`BaseStrategy.should_close`). MAE/MFE are tracked by the simulated broker on every quote.
   6: 
   7: Stop invariants, enforced here before any change:
   8: 
   9: - a position always has a stop: a missing one is re-attached from its intent, or the position is closed;
  10: - the stop only moves in the favourable direction (the rules never propose otherwise; checked again here);
  11: - the new stop keeps at least the broker's stops level (plus the freeze level) from the current price;
  12: - at most one change per position every ``modify_min_interval_seconds``, and only by ``min_sl_step_points``.
  13: """
  14: 
  15: from __future__ import annotations
  16: 
  17: import logging
  18: from collections.abc import Collection, Mapping
  19: from datetime import datetime
  20: 
  21: from app.config import PositionManagementConfig
  22: from app.core.clock import Clock
  23: from app.core.enums import ExitReason, Side
  24: from app.engine.paper import PaperExecution
  25: from app.execution.fill_model import mark_price
  26: from app.execution.management import PositionView, better_stop, from_advice, manage, plan_break_even
  27: from app.market_data.data_models import SymbolSpec
  28: from app.monitoring.alerts import EventBus, EventType
  29: from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy
  30: from app.strategy.signal_models import OpenPosition, StrategyContext
  31: 
  32: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  33: 
  34: 
  35: class PositionManager:
  36:     def __init__(
  37:         self,
  38:         paper: PaperExecution,
  39:         config: PositionManagementConfig,
  40:         specs: Mapping[str, SymbolSpec],
  41:         clock: Clock,
  42:         *,
  43:         strategies_by_magic: Mapping[int, BaseStrategy],
  44:         bus: EventBus | None = None,
  45:     ) -> None:
  46:         self.paper = paper
  47:         self.config = config
  48:         self.specs = dict(specs)
  49:         self.clock = clock
  50:         self.strategies_by_magic = dict(strategies_by_magic)
  51:         self.bus = bus
  52:         self._last_modify: dict[int, datetime] = {}
  53: 
  54:     def _strategy_name(self, magic: int) -> str | None:
  55:         """The strategy that owns a magic number, for its own break-even/trailing (``by_strategy``)."""
  56:         strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(magic)
  57:         return None if strategy is None else strategy.name
  58: 
  59:     def on_quote(self, symbol: str, bid: float, ask: float, atr: float | None) -> None:
  60:         spec = self.specs[symbol]
  61:         now = self.clock.now_utc()
  62:         mine = [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]
  63:         to_break_even = self.paper.plan_parts_closed({p.ticket for p in mine}) if mine else set()
  64:         for pos in mine:
  65:             if pos.close_requested:
  66:                 continue
  67:             if pos.sl is None:
  68:                 self._missing_stop(pos.ticket, pos.request.sl)
  69:                 continue
  70:             if pos.request.sl is None:
  71:                 continue
  72:             mark = mark_price(pos.side, bid, ask - bid)
  73:             name = self._strategy_name(pos.request.magic)
  74:             own = self.config.by_strategy.get(name or "")
  75:             # the paper book counts bars of the main entry timeframe; a lane's strategy counts its own
  76:             bars = (
  77:                 pos.bars_held
  78:                 if own is None or own.timeframe is None
  79:                 else max(0, int((now - pos.entry_time).total_seconds() // own.timeframe.seconds))
  80:             )
  81:             view = PositionView(pos.side, pos.entry_price, pos.request.sl, pos.sl, bars, pos.request.tp)
  82:             cfg = self.config.for_strategy(name)
  83:             adj = manage(view, mark=mark, atr=atr, point=spec.point, cfg=cfg)
  84:             if pos.ticket in to_break_even:
  85:                 plan = plan_break_even(view, mark=mark, point=spec.point, cfg=cfg)
  86:                 adj = better_stop(pos.side, adj, plan)
  87:             if adj.close is not None:
  88:                 self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, adj.close)
  89:                 continue
  90:             if adj.new_sl is None:
  91:                 continue
  92:             if (adj.new_sl - pos.sl) * pos.side.sign <= 0:
  93:                 log.error(
  94:                     "refused an unfavourable stop move on #%s (%s -> %s)", pos.ticket, pos.sl, adj.new_sl
  95:                 )
  96:                 continue
  97:             min_gap = (spec.stops_level + spec.freeze_level) * spec.point
  98:             if (mark - adj.new_sl) * pos.side.sign <= min_gap:
  99:                 continue  # too close to the price for the broker to accept; retry on a later quote
 100:             last = self._last_modify.get(pos.ticket)
 101:             if last is not None and (now - last).total_seconds() < self.config.modify_min_interval_seconds:
 102:                 continue
 103:             old = pos.sl
 104:             self.paper.modify_stop(pos.ticket, adj.new_sl, adj.stop_kind)
 105:             self._last_modify[pos.ticket] = now
 106:             if self.bus is not None:
 107:                 self.bus.emit(
 108:                     EventType.STOP_MOVED,
 109:                     symbol=symbol,
 110:                     ticket=pos.ticket,
 111:                     old=old,
 112:                     new=adj.new_sl,
 113:                     kind=None if adj.stop_kind is None else adj.stop_kind.value,
 114:                     note=adj.note,
 115:                     paper=True,
 116:                 )
 117: 
 118:     def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
 119:         """A new closed entry bar: count it for the time stop and ask the owning strategy about closing."""
 120:         for pos in [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]:
 121:             pos.bars_held += 1
 122:             strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.request.magic)
 123:             if strategy is not None and not pos.close_requested and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
 124:                 log.info("strategy %s closes #%s on %s", strategy.name, pos.ticket, symbol)
 125:                 self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, ExitReason.SIGNAL)
 126: 
 127:     def on_lane_bar(
 128:         self,
 129:         symbol: str,
 130:         ctx: StrategyContext,
 131:         strategies: Collection[str],
 132:         close_check: bool = False,
 133:     ) -> None:
 134:         """A lane's closed entry bar: the own management (``manage_position``) of that lane's strategies,
 135:         and with ``close_check`` their ``should_close`` too. Only the main lane reaches :meth:`on_bar`,
 136:         so without this an extra lane's strategy is never asked to close (found in review 2026-10-27)."""
 137:         spec = self.specs[symbol]
 138:         for pos in [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]:
 139:             strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.request.magic)
 140:             if strategy is None or strategy.name not in strategies or pos.close_requested:
 141:                 continue
 142:             if close_check and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
 143:                 log.info("strategy %s closes #%s on %s (lane bar)", strategy.name, pos.ticket, symbol)
 144:                 self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, ExitReason.SIGNAL)
 145:                 continue
 146:             if pos.sl is None:
 147:                 continue
 148:             mark = ctx.market.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ctx.market.ask
 149:             if mark is None or pos.request.sl is None:
 150:                 continue
 151:             view = OpenPosition(
 152:                 pos.side,
 153:                 pos.entry_price,
 154:                 pos.request.sl,
 155:                 pos.sl,
 156:                 pos.request.tp,
 157:                 pos.entry_time,
 158:                 pos.request.signal_id,
 159:             )
 160:             adj = from_advice(strategy.manage_position(ctx, view), pos.side, mark=mark, sl=pos.sl)
 161:             if adj is None:
 162:                 continue
 163:             if adj.close is not None:
 164:                 log.info("strategy %s closes #%s: %s", strategy.name, pos.ticket, adj.note)
 165:                 self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, adj.close)
 166:             elif adj.new_sl is not None:
 167:                 if (mark - adj.new_sl) * pos.side.sign <= (spec.stops_level + spec.freeze_level) * spec.point:
 168:                     self.paper.request_close(pos.ticket, adj.stop_kind or ExitReason.SIGNAL)
 169:                     continue
 170:                 self.paper.modify_stop(pos.ticket, adj.new_sl, adj.stop_kind)
 171:                 self._last_modify[pos.ticket] = self.clock.now_utc()
 172: 
 173:     def _missing_stop(self, ticket: int, initial: float | None) -> None:
 174:         if initial is not None:
 175:             log.critical("position #%s had no stop; re-attached %s from its intent", ticket, initial)
 176:             self.paper.modify_stop(ticket, initial, None)
 177:         else:
 178:             log.critical("position #%s has no stop and none to re-attach: closing it", ticket)
 179:             self.paper.request_close(ticket, ExitReason.STOP_LOSS)

===== app/engine/broker_positions.py =====
   1: """Broker position management for the DEMO account (PLAN Â§A11; TAA-1205).
   2: 
   3: The same A11 rules as the PAPER manager (:mod:`app.execution.management`), applied to real broker positions
   4: that carry the bot's magic and belong to a known intent (strays are the reconciler's business):
   5: 
   6: - **SL/TP changes** go through ``order_send`` (``TRADE_ACTION_SLTP``). A change is skipped, not forced, when
   7:   the new stop would sit closer to the price than the symbol's stops level, or when the price is inside the
   8:   freeze level of the current stop or target (the server would refuse either). At most one change per
   9:   position every ``modify_min_interval_seconds``, and only by ``min_sl_step_points``; ``10025`` (no changes)
  10:   counts as done.
  11: - **Closes** (time stop, a strategy's close signal) are market deals against the position ticket.
  12: - **Flatten** (kill switch in FLATTEN mode) closes every bot position; it requires
  13:   ``KILL_SWITCH_FLATTEN_ALLOWED=true`` and is refused otherwise.
  14: """
  15: 
  16: from __future__ import annotations
  17: 
  18: import logging
  19: from collections.abc import Collection, Mapping
  20: from datetime import datetime
  21: 
  22: from sqlalchemy import select
  23: 
  24: from app.broker.execution import SendResult
  25: from app.broker.models import BrokerPosition
  26: from app.config import PositionManagementConfig
  27: from app.core.clock import Clock
  28: from app.core.enums import ExitReason, Side, Timeframe
  29: from app.core.errors import SafetyViolation, TaaError
  30: from app.engine.order_manager import OrderManager
  31: from app.execution.management import PositionView, better_stop, from_advice, manage, plan_break_even
  32: from app.market_data.data_models import SymbolSpec
  33: from app.monitoring.alerts import EventBus, EventType
  34: from app.risk.exposure_manager import MAGIC_RANGE
  35: from app.storage.models import OrderIntentRow
  36: from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy
  37: from app.strategy.signal_models import OpenPosition, StrategyContext
  38: 
  39: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  40: 
  41: 
  42: class BrokerPositionManager:
  43:     def __init__(
  44:         self,
  45:         orders: OrderManager,
  46:         config: PositionManagementConfig,
  47:         specs: Mapping[str, SymbolSpec],
  48:         clock: Clock,
  49:         *,
  50:         entry_timeframe: Timeframe,
  51:         magic_base: int,
  52:         strategies_by_magic: Mapping[int, BaseStrategy],
  53:         flatten_allowed: bool,
  54:         bus: EventBus | None = None,
  55:     ) -> None:
  56:         self.orders = orders
  57:         self.config = config
  58:         self.specs = dict(specs)
  59:         self.clock = clock
  60:         self.entry_timeframe = entry_timeframe
  61:         self.magic_base = magic_base
  62:         self.strategies_by_magic = dict(strategies_by_magic)
  63:         self.flatten_allowed = flatten_allowed
  64:         self.bus = bus
  65:         self._last_modify: dict[int, datetime] = {}
  66: 
  67:     # --- lookups ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  68: 
  69:     def bot_positions(self, symbol: str | None = None) -> list[BrokerPosition]:
  70:         try:
  71:             positions = self.orders.market.positions(symbol) if symbol else self.orders.market.positions()
  72:         except TaaError:
  73:             log.warning("positions_get failed; positions are not managed this cycle")
  74:             return []
  75:         return [p for p in positions if self.magic_base <= p.magic < self.magic_base + MAGIC_RANGE]
  76: 
  77:     def initial_stops(self) -> dict[int, float]:
  78:         """Position ticket -> the stop of the intent that opened it (the trade's 1R)."""
  79:         with self.orders.db.session() as sess:
  80:             rows = sess.execute(
  81:                 select(OrderIntentRow.position_ticket, OrderIntentRow.sl).where(
  82:                     OrderIntentRow.position_ticket.is_not(None)
  83:                 )
  84:             ).all()
  85:         return {int(ticket): float(sl) for ticket, sl in rows if ticket is not None}
  86: 
  87:     def _strategy_name(self, magic: int) -> str | None:
  88:         """The strategy that owns a magic number, for its own break-even/trailing (``by_strategy``)."""
  89:         strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(magic)
  90:         return None if strategy is None else strategy.name
  91: 
  92:     def _bars_held(self, position: BrokerPosition) -> int:
  93:         """Bars since the entry on the owning strategy's timeframe (a lane's own, else the entry one)."""
  94:         tf = self.config.bar_timeframe(self._strategy_name(position.magic), self.entry_timeframe)
  95:         held = (self.clock.now_utc() - position.time_utc).total_seconds()
  96:         return max(0, int(held // tf.seconds))
  97: 
  98:     # --- every quote ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  99: 
 100:     def on_quote(self, symbol: str, bid: float, ask: float, atr: float | None) -> None:
 101:         spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
 102:         if spec is None:
 103:             return
 104:         initial = self.initial_stops()
 105:         mine = self.bot_positions(symbol)
 106:         to_break_even = self.orders.plan_parts_closed({p.ticket for p in mine}) if mine else set()
 107:         for pos in mine:
 108:             first_sl = initial.get(pos.ticket)
 109:             if first_sl is None or pos.sl <= 0:
 110:                 continue  # unknown positions and missing stops belong to the reconciler's sweep
 111:             mark = bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ask
 112:             tp = pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None
 113:             view = PositionView(pos.side, pos.price_open, first_sl, pos.sl, self._bars_held(pos), tp)
 114:             cfg = self.config.for_strategy(self._strategy_name(pos.magic))
 115:             adj = manage(view, mark=mark, atr=atr, point=spec.point, cfg=cfg)
 116:             if pos.ticket in to_break_even:
 117:                 plan = plan_break_even(view, mark=mark, point=spec.point, cfg=cfg)
 118:                 adj = better_stop(pos.side, adj, plan)
 119:             if adj.close is not None:
 120:                 self.close(pos, spec, adj.close)
 121:             elif adj.new_sl is not None:
 122:                 self._modify(pos, spec, adj.new_sl, mark, adj.note)
 123: 
 124:     def _modify(self, pos: BrokerPosition, spec: SymbolSpec, new_sl: float, mark: float, note: str) -> None:
 125:         if (new_sl - pos.sl) * pos.side.sign <= 0:
 126:             log.error("refused an unfavourable stop move on #%s (%s -> %s)", pos.ticket, pos.sl, new_sl)
 127:             return
 128:         if (mark - new_sl) * pos.side.sign <= spec.stops_level * spec.point:
 129:             return  # inside the stops level: the server would refuse it; try again on a later quote
 130:         frozen = spec.freeze_level * spec.point
 131:         if frozen and any(level and abs(mark - level) <= frozen for level in (pos.sl, pos.tp)):
 132:             return  # the current stop or target is frozen
 133:         now = self.clock.now_utc()
 134:         last = self._last_modify.get(pos.ticket)
 135:         if last is not None and (now - last).total_seconds() < self.config.modify_min_interval_seconds:
 136:             return
 137:         self._last_modify[pos.ticket] = now
 138:         result = self.orders.execution.send(
 139:             self.orders.builder.modify_stops(spec, pos, new_sl, pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None)
 140:         )
 141:         if result.ok or result.retcode == 10025:
 142:             if self.bus is not None:
 143:                 self.bus.emit(
 144:                     EventType.STOP_MOVED,
 145:                     symbol=pos.symbol,
 146:                     ticket=pos.ticket,
 147:                     old=pos.sl,
 148:                     new=new_sl,
 149:                     note=note,
 150:                     paper=False,
 151:                 )
 152:         else:
 153:             log.warning("stop change on #%s refused: %s", pos.ticket, result.description)
 154:             self.orders.monitor.observe_order_failure(f"modify #{pos.ticket}: {result.description}")
 155: 
 156:     # --- every bar --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 157: 
 158:     def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
 159:         spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
 160:         if spec is None:
 161:             return
 162:         for pos in self.bot_positions(symbol):
 163:             strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.magic)
 164:             if strategy is not None and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
 165:                 self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.SIGNAL)
 166: 
 167:     def on_lane_bar(
 168:         self,
 169:         symbol: str,
 170:         ctx: StrategyContext,
 171:         strategies: Collection[str],
 172:         close_check: bool = False,
 173:     ) -> None:
 174:         """A lane's closed entry bar: the own management of its strategies. The position's signal is not
 175:         known here, so the strategy reads its plan from the position (``OpenPosition.signal_id`` empty).
 176:         With ``close_check`` the strategy is also asked to close: an extra lane never reaches
 177:         :meth:`on_bar`, so its daily flatten would otherwise never fire (review 2026-10-27)."""
 178:         spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
 179:         if spec is None:
 180:             return
 181:         initial = self.initial_stops()
 182:         for pos in self.bot_positions(symbol):
 183:             strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.magic)
 184:             if strategy is None or strategy.name not in strategies:
 185:                 continue
 186:             if close_check and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
 187:                 self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.SIGNAL)
 188:                 continue
 189:             first_sl = initial.get(pos.ticket)
 190:             if first_sl is None or pos.sl <= 0:
 191:                 continue
 192:             mark = ctx.market.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ctx.market.ask
 193:             if mark is None:
 194:                 continue
 195:             tp = pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None
 196:             view = OpenPosition(pos.side, pos.price_open, first_sl, pos.sl, tp, pos.time_utc)
 197:             adj = from_advice(strategy.manage_position(ctx, view), pos.side, mark=mark, sl=pos.sl)
 198:             if adj is None:
 199:                 continue
 200:             if adj.close is not None:
 201:                 self.close(pos, spec, adj.close)
 202:             elif adj.new_sl is not None:
 203:                 self._modify(pos, spec, adj.new_sl, mark, adj.note)
 204: 
 205:     # --- closing ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 206: 
 207:     def close(self, pos: BrokerPosition, spec: SymbolSpec, reason: ExitReason) -> SendResult:
 208:         tick = self.orders.market.tick(pos.symbol)
 209:         price = pos.price_current if tick is None else (tick.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else tick.ask)
 210:         result = self.orders.execution.send(
 211:             self.orders.builder.close(spec, pos, price, comment=f"taa:{reason.value.lower()}")
 212:         )
 213:         if result.ok:
 214:             if self.bus is not None:
 215:                 self.bus.emit(
 216:                     EventType.POSITION_CLOSED,
 217:                     symbol=pos.symbol,
 218:                     ticket=pos.ticket,
 219:                     reason=reason.value,
 220:                     paper=False,
 221:                 )
 222:         else:
 223:             log.error("close of #%s (%s) failed: %s", pos.ticket, reason.value, result.description)
 224:             self.orders.monitor.observe_order_failure(f"close #{pos.ticket}: {result.description}")
 225:         return result
 226: 
 227:     def flatten(self, why: str) -> list[SendResult]:
 228:         """Close every bot position (kill switch FLATTEN). Refused unless explicitly allowed."""
 229:         if not self.flatten_allowed:
 230:             raise SafetyViolation("FLATTEN needs KILL_SWITCH_FLATTEN_ALLOWED=true")
 231:         results = []
 232:         for pos in self.bot_positions():
 233:             spec = self.specs.get(pos.symbol)
 234:             if spec is None:
 235:                 log.error("cannot flatten #%s: no spec for %s", pos.ticket, pos.symbol)
 236:                 continue
 237:             results.append(self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.KILL_SWITCH))
 238:         log.critical("flatten (%s): %d close(s) sent", why, len(results))
 239:         return results

===== app/engine/orchestrator.py =====
   1: """The engine runtime: one loop that monitors, decides and manages (PLAN Â§A2, Â§A19; TAA-601).
   2: 
   3: **Modes:** Milestone 1 runs **PAPER only**: market data from the read-only MT5 gateway, fills on the simulated
   4: broker. BACKTEST runs through ``app.cli backtest``; DEMO and LIVE (broker orders) are Milestone 2 and refused
   5: here.
   6: 
   7: **Schedule** (each step is skipped until it is due; one cycle every ``engine.monitor_interval_seconds``):
   8: 
   9: - every cycle: kill switch, connection (reconnect with backoff), and per symbol a quote â†’ breakers
  10:   (invalid price, spread, staleness) â†’ paper fills â†’ position management;
  11: - every ``candle_poll_seconds``: a newly closed entry bar per symbol (persisted watermark) â†’ context â†’
  12:   strategies â†’ arbitration â†’ decision â†’ paper order;
  13: - every ``health_interval_seconds``: loss tracking and loss breakers, time-based breaker resets, storage and
  14:   disk checks, persisted marks;
  15: - every ``clock_verify_minutes``: server-time verification (an idle market gives no verdict; a mismatch trips
  16:   CLOCK).
  17: 
  18: A failure inside a cycle is logged with its traceback, trips UNHANDLED_EXCEPTION (which blocks entries) and is
  19: announced; the loop keeps monitoring and managing positions. A lost terminal link in the health step's
  20: broker reads (e.g. "No IPC connection" after a laptop sleep) is a connection problem instead: the step skips
  21: them, the next cycle's probe reconnects (the CONNECTION breaker blocks entries meanwhile), and a restored
  22: connection runs the health step (reconcile, book closed trades) and the clock check at once.
  23: :meth:`Engine.stop` ends the loop after the current cycle and shuts down cleanly.
  24: """
  25: 
  26: from __future__ import annotations
  27: 
  28: import dataclasses
  29: import logging
  30: import math
  31: import shutil
  32: import time
  33: from collections.abc import Callable
  34: from dataclasses import dataclass
  35: from datetime import datetime, timedelta
  36: from pathlib import Path
  37: from typing import Any
  38: 
  39: from sqlalchemy import func, select
  40: 
  41: from app.advisory.calibration import CalibrationService

  44: from app.advisory.requirements import (

  48: )

  50: from app.advisory.shadow_tracker import ShadowTracker
  51: from app.advisory.stats import EdgeBook

  53: from app.advisory.universe import SymbolCatalog
  54: from app.ai.gate import AIGate
  55: from app.ai.notes import AINotes

  99: from app.risk.breaker_monitor import BreakerMonitor
 100: from app.risk.circuit_breaker import BreakerBoard, default_specs
 101: from app.risk.exposure_manager import MAGIC_RANGE, Exposure, ExposureManager, PositionRisk
 102: from app.risk.kill_switch import KillMode, KillSwitch
 103: from app.risk.limits import ProfileLimits
 104: from app.risk.loss_tracker import LossStatus, LossTracker
 105: from app.risk.mode_gates import GateResult, evaluate_gate
 106: from app.storage.audit import AuditLog
 107: from app.storage.database import Database
 108: from app.storage.models import ManualTradeLinkRow, RiskDeal
 109: from app.storage.repositories import EngineStateRepository, RunRepository
 110: from app.strategy.ai_lane import AIEntry, AITrader, AITraderParams, Held
 111: from app.strategy.arbitration import SignalArbiter
 112: from app.strategy.catalog import default_registry
 113: from app.strategy.context_builder import ContextBuilder
 114: from app.strategy.release_straddle import OwnS2, StraddleEntry
 115: from app.strategy.setups import EvidenceSetup
 116: from app.strategy.signal_models import Signal, StrategyContext
 117: from app.sync.candles import CandleStreamer
 118: from app.sync.commands import Command, CommandFailed, CommandProcessor, CommandType, Handler, drain
 119: from app.sync.heartbeat import (
 120:     MAX_FOREIGN_POSITIONS,
 121:     MAX_FORMING,
 122:     MAX_QUOTES,
 123:     HeartbeatEmitter,
 124:     market_state,
 125: )
 126: from app.sync.replication import Replicator, install_replication
 127: from app.sync.runtime import SyncRuntime
 128: 
 129: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 130: 
 131: DISABLED_KEY = "disabled_strategies"
 132: SNAPSHOT_KEY = "sync_snapshot"  # set once every replicated row has been queued for the cloud
 133: FAILED_STOP_WINDOW = timedelta(minutes=10)  # a stop this soon after a failed cycle counts as a failure
 134: # the accepted entries a strategy's entry_group reads (PLAN-1 B2's morning)
 135: ENTRY_LOOKBACK = timedelta(days=1)
 136: QUOTE_POLL_SECONDS = 0.1  # a release straddle reads quotes this often around its release (S2: fills at ~1 s)
 137: 
 138: 
 139: LIVE_BANNER = """
 140: ########################################################################################################
 141: ##  LIVE TRADING: REAL MONEY. Orders go to account %s on %s (%s, equity %s).
 142: ##  Every order passes the live gate (6 conditions); the kill switch stops new orders at once:
 143: ##  python -m app.cli kill --reason "..."
 144: ########################################################################################################"""
 145: 
 146: 
 147: @dataclass
 148: class _Due:
 149:     """A periodic task's next due time (monotonic seconds)."""
 150: 
 151:     every: float
 152:     next_at: float = 0.0
 153: 
 154:     def due(self, now: float) -> bool:
 155:         if now >= self.next_at:
 156:             self.next_at = now + self.every
 157:             return True
 158:         return False
 159: 
 160: 
 161: class Engine:
 162:     def __init__(
 163:         self,
 164:         settings: Settings,
 165:         bundle: BrokerBundle,
 166:         db: Database,
 167:         clock: Clock,
 168:         *,
 169:         bus: EventBus,
 170:         sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
 171:         process: str = "engine",
 172:         sync: SyncRuntime | None = None,
 173:     ) -> None:
 174:         if settings.mode is TradingMode.DEMO:
 175:             if not (settings.env.ENABLE_DEMO_TRADING and bundle.client.allow_trading):
 176:                 raise SafetyViolation(
 177:                     "DEMO needs ENABLE_DEMO_TRADING=true and a trading client (build_trading)"
 178:                 )
 179:         elif settings.mode is TradingMode.LIVE:
 180:             # build_trading checked the flag and the account-bound phrase; the live gate checks them again,
 181:             # with the account, terminal, kill switch and breakers, before every order (PLAN Â§A3; TAA-1401)
 182:             if not (settings.env.ENABLE_LIVE_TRADING and bundle.client.allow_trading):
 183:                 raise SafetyViolation(
 184:                     "LIVE needs ENABLE_LIVE_TRADING=true and a trading client (build_trading)"
 185:                 )
 186:         elif settings.mode is not TradingMode.PAPER:
 187:             raise SafetyViolation(
 188:                 f"the engine runs PAPER, DEMO or LIVE (TRADING_MODE={settings.mode.value}); backtests run "
 189:                 "with `python -m app.cli backtest`"
 190:             )
 191:         self.settings = settings
 192:         self.config = settings.config
 193:         self.bundle = bundle
 194:         self.gateway = bundle.gateway
 195:         self.db = db
 196:         self.clock = clock
 197:         self.bus = bus
 198:         self.sleep = sleep
 199:         self.process = process
 200:         self.run_id = new_id()
 201:         self.running = False
 202:         self.cycles = 0
 203:         self.symbols: dict[str, SymbolSpec] = {}
 204:         self.last_error: str = ""
 205:         self._error_at: datetime | None = None  # the last failed cycle (wall clock)
 206:         self._clock_ok = False
 207:         self._kill_active = False
 208:         self._connected = True
 209:         self._atr: dict[str, float] = {}
 210:         self._last_entry: dict[str, datetime] = {}
 211:         self._booked_deals = 0
 212:         self.ranking: RankingService | None = None
 213:         self.scanner: OpportunityScanner | None = None
 214:         self.ai_notes: AINotes | None = None
 215:         self.ai_trader: AITraderLane | None = None
 216:         self.lifecycle: OpportunityLifecycle | None = None
 217:         self.shadow: ShadowTracker | None = None
 218:         self.calibration: CalibrationService | None = None
 219:         self.sync = sync  # cloud replication; None unless sync.enabled (built in start())
 220:         self.commands: CommandProcessor | None = None  # remote commands, with sync
 221:         self.candle_stream: CandleStreamer | None = None  # closed bars to the cloud, with sync (TAA-706)
 222:         self.heartbeats: HeartbeatEmitter | None = None  # liveness + market state to the cloud (TAA-705)
 223:         # the owner's profile inside the local cage (TAA-710)
 224:         self.risk_limits: RiskLimitSelector | None = None
 225:         self.manual_links = ManualTradeLinker(db, clock)  # manual MT5 trades matched to signals (TAA-1006)
 226:         self._last_quotes: dict[str, Quote] = {}
 227:         self._last_loss: LossStatus | None = None  # the health step's, for the cloud heartbeat (TAA-904)
 228:         self._currency = ""
 229:         self.resync_requested = False
 230:         self._requirements_cache: tuple[tuple[datetime | None, str | None], ComputeRequirements] | None = None
 231:         loop = self.config.engine
 232:         self._candles_due = _Due(loop.candle_poll_seconds)
 233:         self._health_due = _Due(loop.health_interval_seconds)
 234:         self._clock_due = _Due(loop.clock_verify_minutes * 60)
 235:         self.audit = AuditLog(db, f"engine:{settings.env.ENGINE_ID or 'local'}", clock)
 236:         # trade lifecycle on the audit chain (TAA-907); one such sink per bus, also when an engine is rebuilt
 237:         self.bus.sinks = [s for s in self.bus.sinks if not isinstance(s, TradeAuditSink)]
 238:         self.bus.sinks.append(TradeAuditSink(self.audit))
 239:         # Replication starts before anything is written, so every row change of this run reaches the outbox.
 240:         self.replicator: Replicator | None = (
 241:             install_replication(db, clock, self.audit.chain)
 242:             if sync is not None or self.config.sync.enabled
 243:             else None
 244:         )
 245:         self.heartbeat_path = settings.path(loop.heartbeat_file)
 246:         self.runs = RunRepository(db, clock)
 247:
