  51:     box: float = Field(default=0.33, gt=0, le=1)
  52:     break_even: bool = True
  53: 
  54: 
  55: def in_window(at: pd.Timestamp) -> bool:
  56:     """Research windows use a start-exclusive, end-inclusive close timestamp."""
  57:     utc = at.tz_convert("UTC")
  58:     bangkok = utc.tz_convert(BANGKOK)
  59:     new_york = utc.tz_convert(NEW_YORK)
  60:     bt, nt = bangkok.time(), new_york.time()
  61:     return (time(7) < bt <= time(9)) or (time(13) < bt <= time(15)) or (time(8) < nt <= time(11, 30))
  62: 
  63: 
  64: def stepped(swings: list[Swing]) -> str:
  65:     highs = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == 1]
  66:     lows = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == -1]
  67:     if len(highs) < 2 or len(lows) < 2:
  68:         return "FLAT"
  69:     if highs[-1] < highs[-2] and lows[-1] < lows[-2]:
  70:         return "DOWN"
  71:     if highs[-1] > highs[-2] and lows[-1] > lows[-2]:
  72:         return "UP"
  73:     return "FLAT"
  74: 
  75: 
  76: def running_push_exceeded(run: float, risk: float) -> bool:
  77:     """Apply the measured strict 1.2 R cutoff to the preceding M1 run."""
  78:     return run > 1.2 * risk
  79: 
  80: 
  81: def break_even_advice(ctx: StrategyContext, pos: OpenPosition) -> PositionAdvice | None:
  82:     """Replay closed M1 bars so a stop crossed before a threshold touch keeps precedence."""
  83:     if (pos.side is Side.BUY and pos.sl >= pos.entry) or (pos.side is Side.SELL and pos.sl <= pos.entry):
  84:         return None
  85:     m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1)
  86:     bars = m1[(m1.index >= pd.Timestamp(pos.entry_time)) & (m1.index <= pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc))]
  87:     risk = abs(pos.initial_sl - pos.entry)
  88:     if risk <= 0 or bars.empty:
  89:         return None
  90:     sell = pos.side is Side.SELL
  91:     stop = pos.sl
  92:     moved = False
  93:     highs = bars["high"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
  94:     lows = bars["low"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
  95:     for high, low in zip(highs, lows, strict=True):
  96:         if (high >= stop) if sell else (low <= stop):
  97:             return None
  98:         if pos.tp is not None and ((low <= pos.tp) if sell else (high >= pos.tp)):
  99:             return None
 100:         ahead = pos.entry - low if sell else high - pos.entry
 101:         if ahead >= 0.5 * risk:
 102:             stop = pos.entry
 103:             moved = True
 104:     if not moved:
 105:         return None
 106:     return PositionAdvice(new_sl=pos.entry, stop_kind=ExitReason.BREAK_EVEN, note="+0.5 R touched")
 107: 
 108: 
 109: class TrendBox(BaseStrategy):
 110:     """Shared confirmed H1 swing, M5 box and running-push gates."""
 111: 
 112:     Params = TrendBoxParams
 113:     direction: str
 114: 
 115:     def required_timeframes(self) -> tuple[Timeframe, ...]:
 116:         return Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1
 117: 
 118:     def warmup_bars(self) -> int:
 119:         return 1200 if self.params.require_big_tf or self.params.require_edge_side else 400
 120: 
 121:     def evaluate(self, ctx: StrategyContext) -> Signal:
 122:         at = pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc)
 123:         if self.params.require_edge_side:
 124:             if not in_window(at):
 125:                 return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.OUTSIDE_SESSION)
 126:             side = edge_side(ctx.frames, at, ctx.market.entry_timeframe)
 127:             expected = Side.SELL if self.direction == "DOWN" else Side.BUY
 128:             if side is not expected:
 129:                 return self.hold(ctx, "AGAINST_EDGE_SIDE")
 130: 
 131:         h1_frame = ctx.frame(Timeframe.H1)
 132:         h1 = h1_frame.iloc[-400:]
 133:         m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5)
 134:         m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1)
 135:         if len(h1) < 400 or len(m5) < 25 or not len(m1):
 136:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
 137:         if not self.params.require_edge_side and not in_window(at):
 138:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.OUTSIDE_SESSION)
 139:         swings, _forming = zigzag(h1.high.to_numpy(float), h1.low.to_numpy(float))
 140:         highs = [s for s in swings if s.kind == 1]
 141:         lows = [s for s in swings if s.kind == -1]
 142:         down = self.direction == "DOWN"
 143:         stepping = (
 144:             len(highs) >= 2
 145:             and len(lows) >= 2
 146:             and (
 147:                 (highs[-1].price < highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price < lows[-2].price)
 148:                 if down
 149:                 else (highs[-1].price > highs[-2].price and lows[-1].price > lows[-2].price)
 150:             )
 151:         )
 152:         if not stepping or len(swings) < 2:
 153:             return self.hold(ctx, "TREND_STEPPING")
 154:         if self.params.require_big_tf:
 155:             # Imported at use time because range_break reuses the trend box helpers above.
 156:             from app.strategy.range_break import RangeBreak
 157: 
 158:             h4 = RangeBreak._h4_from_h1(h1_frame)
 159:             h4 = h4[(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)) <= at].iloc[-300:]
 160:             if len(h4) < 300:
 161:                 return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
 162:             h4_swings, _h4_forming = zigzag(h4.high.to_numpy(float), h4.low.to_numpy(float))
 163:             h4_direction = stepped(h4_swings)
 164:             if (down and h4_direction == "UP") or (not down and h4_direction == "DOWN"):
 165:                 return self.hold(ctx, "H4_AGAINST")
 166:         a, b = swings[-2:]
 167:         if (a.kind, b.kind) != ((1, -1) if down else (-1, 1)):
 168:             return self.hold(ctx, "LEG_ORDER")
 169:         leg = abs(a.price - b.price)
 170:         if leg <= 0:
 171:             return self.hold(ctx, "ZERO_LEG")
 172:         decision = m5.iloc[-1]
 173:         price = float(decision.close)
 174:         selected: tuple[int, float, float] | None = None
 175:         for n in range(24, 5, -1):
 176:             if len(m5) < n + 1:
 177:                 continue
 178:             box = m5.iloc[-n - 1 : -1]
 179:             top, floor = float(box.high.max()), float(box.low.min())
 180:             if top - floor <= self.params.box * leg:
 181:                 selected = n, top, floor
 182:                 break
 183:         if selected is None:
 184:             return self.hold(ctx, "NO_BOX")
 185:         _n, top, floor = selected
 186:         mid = (top + floor) / 2
 187:         retr = (mid - b.price) / leg if down else (b.price - mid) / leg
 188:         if not 0.382 <= retr <= 0.786:
 189:             return self.hold(ctx, "RETRACE")
 190:         if (down and price >= floor) or (not down and price <= top):
 191:             return self.hold(ctx, "NO_CLOSE_BREAK")
 192:         stop = (
 193:             top + self.params.buffer * (top - floor) if down else floor - self.params.buffer * (top - floor)
 194:         )
 195:         stop_floor = 0.0
 196:         stop_widened = False
 197:         if self.params.min_stop_atr > 0:
 198:             closed_h1 = h1_frame[h1_frame.index <= at]
 199:             atr_values = atr(closed_h1.high, closed_h1.low, closed_h1.close, 14)
 200:             atr_value = float(atr_values.iloc[-1]) if len(atr_values) else float("nan")
 201:             if pd.isna(atr_value):
 202:                 return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
 203:             stop_floor = self.params.min_stop_atr * atr_value
 204:             if abs(stop - price) < stop_floor:
 205:                 stop = price + stop_floor if down else price - stop_floor
 206:                 stop_widened = True
 207:         target = b.price
 208:         risk, reward = abs(stop - price), abs(target - price)
 209:         if reward < self.params.min_take_profit or reward < self.params.min_r * risk:
 210:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.RR_TOO_LOW)
 211:         if (down and target >= price) or (not down and target <= price) or risk <= 0:
 212:             return self.hold(ctx, "INVALID_GEOMETRY")
 213:         run_frame = m1[(m1.index >= at - pd.Timedelta(minutes=60)) & (m1.index < at)]
 214:         if run_frame.empty:
 215:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
 216:         run = price - float(run_frame.low.min()) if down else float(run_frame.high.max()) - price
 217:         if running_push_exceeded(run, risk):
 218:             return self.hold(ctx, "RUNNING_PUSH")
 219:         action = Action.SELL if down else Action.BUY
 220:         conditions = [
 221:             Condition("stepping", True),
 222:             Condition("box", True, detail=f"{_n} bars"),
 223:             Condition("retrace", True, detail=f"{retr:.3f}"),
 224:             Condition("trigger", True),
 225:             Condition("run_gate", True, detail=f"{run:.2f} <= {1.2 * risk:.2f}"),
 226:         ]
 227:         if stop_widened:
 228:             conditions.append(Condition("stop_floor", True, detail=f"{stop_floor:.2f}"))
 229:         return self.entry(
 230:             ctx,
 231:             action,
 232:             entry_price=price,
 233:             stop_loss=stop,
 234:             take_profit=target,
 235:             conditions=conditions,
 236:             score=80,
 237:             explanation=f"{self.direction.lower()} confirmed-swing box break",
 238:         )
 239: 
 240:     def should_close(self, ctx: StrategyContext, side: Side) -> bool:
 241:         at = pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc).tz_convert(BANGKOK)
 242:         return at.time() == time(7)
 243: 
 244:     def manage_position(self, ctx: StrategyContext, pos: OpenPosition) -> PositionAdvice | None:
 245:         if not self.params.break_even:
 246:             return None
 247:         return break_even_advice(ctx, pos)
 248: 
 249: 
 250: class TrendBoxDown(TrendBox):
 251:     name = "trend_box_down"
 252:     Params = TrendBoxDownParams
 253:     direction = "DOWN"
 254: 
 255: 
 256: class TrendBoxUp(TrendBox):
 257:     name = "trend_box_up"
 258:     Params = TrendBoxUpParams
 259:     direction = "UP"

===== app/strategy/range_break.py =====
   1: """H4 confirmed-swing range breaks on M5 closes."""
   2: 
   3: from __future__ import annotations
   4: 
   5: from datetime import time
   6: from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
   7: 
   8: import pandas as pd
   9: from pydantic import Field
  10: 
  11: from app.core.enums import Action, Side, Timeframe
  12: from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy, StrategyParams
  13: from app.strategy.edge_side import edge_side
  14: from app.strategy.signal_models import (
  15:     Condition,
  16:     OpenPosition,
  17:     PositionAdvice,
  18:     ReasonCode,
  19:     Signal,
  20:     StrategyContext,
  21: )
  22: from app.strategy.trend_box import break_even_advice, in_window, running_push_exceeded, stepped
  23: from app.strategy.zigzag import zigzag
  24: 
  25: BANGKOK = ZoneInfo("Asia/Bangkok")
  26: 
  27: 
  28: class RangeBreakParams(StrategyParams):
  29:     """Measured breakout plan constants."""
  30: 
  31:     buffer: float = Field(default=0.3, ge=0, le=2)
  32:     tp_range: float = Field(default=0.5, gt=0, le=5)
  33:     min_r: float = Field(default=0.8, gt=0, le=10)
  34:     # Owner 2026-10-27: "you can take the 5 USD out". Measured first (research/.../gate_bite.py): the
  35:     # 5.00 USD floor rejected 1 of 496 complete signals, because every target here is a swing or half an
  36:     # H4 box. The gate stays so a lane can set one; 0.0 is off.
  37:     min_take_profit: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0)
  38:     require_edge_side: bool = False
  39: 
  40: 
  41: class RangeBreak(BaseStrategy):
  42:     name = "range_break"
  43:     Params = RangeBreakParams
  44: 
  45:     def required_timeframes(self) -> tuple[Timeframe, ...]:
  46:         return Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1
  47: 
  48:     def warmup_bars(self) -> int:
  49:         # H1 bars, not H4: the 300 H4 bars the box is read from are resampled from 1,200 closed H1 bars.
  50:         # Declaring 300 here would let a lane configure 300 H1 bars, give the ZigZag 75 H4 bars instead of
  51:         # 300, and change the box silently (found in review, 2026-10-27).
  52:         return 1200
  53: 
  54:     @staticmethod
  55:     def _h4_from_h1(h1: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
  56:         # App frames are indexed by H1 close; the research resamples bars indexed by H1 open.
  57:         opens = h1.copy()
  58:         opens.index = pd.DatetimeIndex(opens.index) - pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
  59:         return (
  60:             opens.resample("4h").agg({"open": "first", "high": "max", "low": "min", "close": "last"}).dropna()
  61:         )
  62: 
  63:     def evaluate(self, ctx: StrategyContext) -> Signal:
  64:         at = pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc)
  65:         m5, m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5), ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1)
  66:         h1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.H1).iloc[-1200:]
  67:         h4 = self._h4_from_h1(h1)
  68:         h4 = h4[(h4.index + pd.Timedelta(hours=4)) <= at].iloc[-300:]
  69:         if len(h4) < 300 or len(m5) < 2 or not len(m1):
  70:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
  71:         if not in_window(at):
  72:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.OUTSIDE_SESSION)
  73:         swings, _forming = zigzag(h4.high.to_numpy(float), h4.low.to_numpy(float))
  74:         highs = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == 1]
  75:         lows = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == -1]
  76:         if stepped(swings) != "FLAT" or not highs or not lows:
  77:             return self.hold(ctx, "H4_STEPPING")
  78:         hh, ll = highs[-1], lows[-1]
  79:         if hh <= ll:
  80:             return self.hold(ctx, "INVALID_H4_BOX")
  81:         prev, price = float(m5.close.iloc[-2]), float(m5.close.iloc[-1])
  82:         rng = hh - ll
  83:         if price > hh >= prev:
  84:             side, stop, target = (
  85:                 Side.BUY,
  86:                 hh - self.params.buffer * rng * 0.25,
  87:                 price + self.params.tp_range * rng,
  88:             )
  89:         elif price < ll <= prev:
  90:             side, stop, target = (
  91:                 Side.SELL,
  92:                 ll + self.params.buffer * rng * 0.25,
  93:                 price - self.params.tp_range * rng,
  94:             )
  95:         else:
  96:             return self.hold(ctx, "NO_CLOSE_BREAK")
  97:         if self.params.require_edge_side:
  98:             edge = edge_side(ctx.frames, at, ctx.market.entry_timeframe)
  99:             if edge is not side:
 100:                 return self.hold(ctx, "AGAINST_EDGE_SIDE")
 101:         risk, reward = abs(stop - price), abs(target - price)
 102:         if risk <= 0 or reward < self.params.min_take_profit or reward < self.params.min_r * risk:
 103:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.RR_TOO_LOW)
 104:         run_frame = m1[(m1.index >= at - pd.Timedelta(minutes=60)) & (m1.index < at)]
 105:         if run_frame.empty:
 106:             return self.hold(ctx, ReasonCode.INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
 107:         run = float(run_frame.high.max()) - price if side is Side.BUY else price - float(run_frame.low.min())
 108:         if running_push_exceeded(run, risk):
 109:             return self.hold(ctx, "RUNNING_PUSH")
 110:         return self.entry(
 111:             ctx,
 112:             Action.BUY if side is Side.BUY else Action.SELL,
 113:             entry_price=price,
 114:             stop_loss=stop,
 115:             take_profit=target,
 116:             conditions=(
 117:                 Condition("h4_flat", True),
 118:                 Condition("range_break", True),
 119:                 Condition("run_gate", True),
 120:             ),
 121:             score=80,
 122:             explanation="confirmed H4 range break",
 123:         )
 124: 
 125:     def should_close(self, ctx: StrategyContext, side: Side) -> bool:
 126:         return pd.Timestamp(ctx.decision_time_utc).tz_convert(BANGKOK).time() == time(7)
 127: 
 128:     def manage_position(self, ctx: StrategyContext, pos: OpenPosition) -> PositionAdvice | None:
 129:         return break_even_advice(ctx, pos)

===== app/indicators/volatility.py =====
   1: """Volatility indicators: ATR, Bollinger Bands, historical volatility and ATR percentile (PLAN Â§A6)."""
   2: 
   3: from __future__ import annotations
   4: 
   5: import math
   6: 
   7: import numpy as np
   8: import pandas as pd
   9: from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import sliding_window_view
  10: 
  11: from app.core.errors import ConfigError
  12: from app.indicators.common import (
  13:     as_float,
  14:     check_aligned,
  15:     check_period,
  16:     rolling_mean,
  17:     to_series,
  18:     true_range_values,
  19:     wilder_values,
  20: )
  21: 
  22: 
  23: def true_range(high: pd.Series, low: pd.Series, close: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
  24:     """``max(H - L, |H - C[t-1]|, |L - C[t-1]|)``; position 0 has no previous close and is NaN."""
  25:     check_aligned(high, low, close)
  26:     return to_series(true_range_values(as_float(high), as_float(low), as_float(close)), close, "true_range")
  27: 
  28: 
  29: def atr(high: pd.Series, low: pd.Series, close: pd.Series, n: int = 14) -> pd.Series:
  30:     """Wilder-smoothed true range, in price units; first defined at position *n*."""
  31:     check_period("n", n)
  32:     check_aligned(high, low, close)
  33:     tr = true_range_values(as_float(high), as_float(low), as_float(close))
  34:     return to_series(wilder_values(tr, n), close, f"atr_{n}")
  35: 
  36: 
  37: def bollinger(close: pd.Series, n: int = 20, k: float = 2.0) -> pd.DataFrame:
  38:     """Columns ``mid`` (SMA), ``upper``/``lower`` (mid Â± kÂ·Ïƒ, population Ïƒ), ``width`` and ``percent_b``.
  39: 
  40:     ``width = (upper - lower) / mid``; ``percent_b = (C - lower) / (upper - lower)``, NaN for a flat window.
  41:     All columns are first defined at ``n - 1``.
  42:     """
  43:     check_period("n", n, minimum=2)
  44:     if not (math.isfinite(k) and k > 0):
  45:         raise ConfigError(f"Bollinger k must be > 0 (got {k!r})")
  46:     c = as_float(close)
  47:     mid = rolling_mean(c, n)
  48:     sigma = np.full(c.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)
  49:     flat = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=bool)
  50:     if len(c) >= n:
  51:         windows = sliding_window_view(c, n)
  52:         sigma[n - 1 :] = windows.std(axis=1, ddof=0)
  53:         # exact flatness test: rolling float sums leave rounding noise where Ïƒ should be 0
  54:         flat[n - 1 :] = np.ptp(windows, axis=1) == 0
  55:     sigma[flat] = 0.0
  56:     upper, lower = mid + k * sigma, mid - k * sigma
  57:     band = upper - lower
  58:     with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
  59:         width = np.where(mid > 0, band / mid, np.nan)
  60:         percent_b = np.where(band > 0, (c - lower) / band, np.nan)
  61:     return pd.DataFrame(
  62:         {"mid": mid, "upper": upper, "lower": lower, "width": width, "percent_b": percent_b},
  63:         index=close.index,
  64:     )
  65: 
  66: 
  67: def historical_volatility(close: pd.Series, n: int = 20, *, bars_per_year: float) -> pd.Series:
  68:     """Annualized volatility: sample stdev (ddof=1) of the last *n* log returns Ã— âˆšbars_per_year.
  69: 
  70:     *bars_per_year* depends on the timeframe and the market's trading hours (``Timeframe.bars_per_year``
  71:     assumes a 24x5 market; crypto trades 24x7). A decimal fraction (0.12 = 12 %); first defined at
  72:     position *n*. Non-positive prices are NaN.
  73:     """
  74:     check_period("n", n, minimum=2)
  75:     if not (math.isfinite(bars_per_year) and bars_per_year > 0):
  76:         raise ConfigError(f"bars_per_year must be > 0 (got {bars_per_year!r})")
  77:     c = as_float(close)
  78:     with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
  79:         log_c = np.where(c > 0, np.log(c), np.nan)
  80:     returns = np.diff(log_c, prepend=np.nan)
  81:     std = pd.Series(returns).rolling(n, min_periods=n).std(ddof=1).to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
  82:     return to_series(std * math.sqrt(bars_per_year), close, f"hv_{n}")
  83: 
  84: 
  85: def atr_percentile(atr_values: pd.Series, lookback: int = 100) -> pd.Series:
  86:     """Where the current ATR sits among the previous ``lookback - 1`` values, in [0, 100].
  87: 
  88:     ``100 Â· count(prev â‰¤ current) / (lookback - 1)`` over the window ending at *t*: 100 means the highest
  89:     ATR of the window, 0 the lowest. Defined once *lookback* consecutive finite ATR values exist
  90:     (for ATR(n): at position ``n + lookback - 1``).
  91:     """
  92:     check_period("lookback", lookback, minimum=2)
  93:     a = as_float(atr_values)
  94:     out = np.full(a.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)
  95:     if len(a) >= lookback:
  96:         windows = sliding_window_view(a, lookback)
  97:         current = windows[:, -1]
  98:         counts = (windows[:, :-1] <= current[:, None]).sum(axis=1)
  99:         pct = 100.0 * counts / (lookback - 1)
 100:         complete = np.isfinite(windows).all(axis=1)
 101:         out[lookback - 1 :] = np.where(complete, pct, np.nan)
 102:     return to_series(out, atr_values, f"atr_pct_{lookback}")

===== app/engine/ai_trader.py =====
   1: """The AI trader lane in the engine (PLAN Â§A37, TAA-1805): on each closed M1 bar of one symbol the engine
   2: hands the bars to the AI watcher (app/ai/watcher.py), which wakes the AI trader (app/ai/trader.py) when
   3: something happens - the very modules the research's watcher and replays run.
   4: 
   5: The bars are fetched on the engine loop (the MT5 client is single-threaded); the watcher's cycle, which may
   6: wait 5-15 s for the AI, runs on one background thread, so the loop never waits for it. A new bar while a call
   7: is still running is not lost: its closes are carried to the next cycle. In TAA-1805 the decisions are recorded
   8: only; TAA-1806 turns them into signals through the decision engine.
   9: """
  10: 
  11: from __future__ import annotations
  12: 
  13: import json
  14: import logging
  15: from concurrent.futures import Future, ThreadPoolExecutor
  16: from datetime import datetime, timedelta
  17: from pathlib import Path
  18: from typing import Any, Protocol
  19: 
  20: import pandas as pd
  21: 
  22: from app.ai import plan as day_plan
  23: from app.ai import trader
  24: from app.ai.providers import AIProvider
  25: from app.ai.watcher import AIWatcher
  26: from app.config import AITraderConfig
  27: from app.core.enums import Side, Timeframe
  28: from app.market_data.data_models import CandleFrame
  29: from app.news.releases import Release
  30: from app.strategy import knowledge as kn
  31: from app.strategy.ai_lane import AIOrder, AITrader
  32: 
  33: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  34: 
  35: # closed bars handed to the readers per frame: M5 from 1,400 x 5 M1 bars (rhythm), 2,600 bars of the rest, the
  36: # D1 history for the zone grid's anchors (research/2026-10-23-entry: rhythm_scan, picture_read, knowledge)
  37: TH_ZONE = "Asia/Bangkok"
  38: COUNTS = {Timeframe.M1: 7200, Timeframe.M15: 2700, Timeframe.H1: 2700, Timeframe.H4: 2700, Timeframe.D1: 2500}
  39: 
  40: 
  41: class ClosedCandles(Protocol):
  42:     def closed_candles(self, symbol: str, tf: Timeframe, count: int) -> CandleFrame: ...
  43: 
  44: 
  45: def position_note(
  46:     positions: list[tuple[Side, float, float | None, float | None, datetime]], pending: AIOrder | None
  47: ) -> str:
  48:     """What the system holds now, for the AI (2026-07-07 replay: it answered HOLD for an order that never
  49:     filled and EXIT with nothing open): the open positions (side, entry, stop, target, opened) and the order
  50:     waiting for its price."""
  51:     lines = ["THE SYSTEM'S POSITION NOW (from the broker; HOLD / EXIT only for these):"]
  52:     for side, entry, sl, tp, opened in positions:
  53:         lines.append(
  54:             f"- open {side.value} from {entry:.2f}, stop {sl}, target {tp}, opened "
  55:             f"{pd.Timestamp(opened).tz_convert(TH_ZONE):%H:%M} Thai"
  56:         )
  57:     if pending is not None:
  58:         lines.append(
  59:             f"- waiting order {pending.side.value} at {pending.entry:.2f}, stop {pending.stop}, target "
  60:             f"{pending.target}, until {pd.Timestamp(pending.valid_until).tz_convert(TH_ZONE):%H:%M} Thai"
  61:         )
  62:     if len(lines) == 1:
  63:         lines.append("- none: no open position and no waiting order")
  64:     return "\n".join(lines)
  65: 
  66: 
  67: def to_frame(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
  68:     """Candle rows (``CANDLE_COLUMNS``) as the readers take them: indexed by the bar's open (UTC), the last
  69:     copy of a bar kept - the same shape as the research's stored history (research zone_wait.load)."""
  70:     out = df.assign(t=pd.to_datetime(df["open_time"], utc=True)).drop_duplicates("t", keep="last")
  71:     return out.set_index("t").sort_index()[["open", "high", "low", "close", "tick_volume"]]
  72: 
  73: 
  74: class AITraderLane:
  75:     def __init__(
  76:         self,
  77:         cfg: AITraderConfig,
  78:         candles: ClosedCandles,
  79:         provider: AIProvider,
  80:         releases: tuple[Release, ...],
  81:         store: Path,
  82:         journal: Path,
  83:         *,
  84:         background: bool = True,
  85:     ) -> None:
  86:         self.cfg = cfg
  87:         self.candles = candles
  88:         self.provider = provider
  89:         self.releases = releases
  90:         self.store = store
  91:         self.journal_path = journal
  92:         self._frames: kn.Frames | None = None
  93:         self._last_open: pd.Timestamp | None = None
  94:         self._checked: pd.Timestamp | None = None
  95:         self._executor = (
  96:             ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="ai-trader") if background else None
  97:         )
  98:         self._running: Future[dict[str, Any] | None] | None = None
  99:         self.cycles = 0
 100:         self._decision: dict[str, Any] | None = None  # the last finished decision, not yet handed over
 101:         self._plan_orders: list[AIOrder] | None = None  # the last plan's orders, not yet handed over
 102:         self.position = position_note([], None)  # set by the engine before each tick
 103:         self.watcher = AIWatcher(
 104:             store,
 105:             frames=self._current_frames,
 106:             decide=self._decide,
 107:             journal=self._journal,
 108:             cap_usd=cfg.daily_cap_usd,
 109:             model=cfg.model,
 110:             plan=self._plan,
 111:             holding=lambda: "- none:" not in self.position,
 112:         )
 113: 
 114:     # --- the engine loop ---
 115: 
 116:     def tick(self) -> bool:
 117:         """Called on every engine cycle; starts at most one watcher cycle per new closed M1 bar, never waits.
 118:         Returns True when one was started."""
 119:         if self._running is not None:
 120:             if not self._running.done():
 121:                 return False
 122:             self._decision = self._running.result() or self._decision  # the watcher never raises
 123:             self._running = None
 124:         m1 = self.candles.closed_candles(self.cfg.symbol, Timeframe.M1, 2)
 125:         if m1.last_open_time is None:
 126:             return False
 127:         opened = pd.Timestamp(m1.last_open_time)
 128:         opened = opened.tz_localize("UTC") if opened.tzinfo is None else opened.tz_convert("UTC")
 129:         if self._last_open is not None and opened <= self._last_open:
 130:             return False
 131:         self._frames = self.frames()
 132:         now = opened + pd.Timedelta(minutes=1)  # the bar's close: everything read is closed by it
 133:         since = self._checked if self._checked is not None else opened
 134:         closes = [float(x) for x in self._frames.m1[self._frames.m1.index >= since].close]
 135:         self._last_open, self._checked = opened, now
 136:         self.cycles += 1
 137:         if self._executor is None:
 138:             self._decision = self.watcher.on_bar(now, closes) or self._decision
 139:         else:
 140:             self._running = self._executor.submit(self.watcher.on_bar, now, closes)
 141:         return True
 142: 
 143:     @property
 144:     def m1(self) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
 145:         """The closed M1 bars of the last watcher cycle (None before the first)."""
 146:         return None if self._frames is None else self._frames.m1
 147: 
 148:     def take_decision(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
 149:         """The AI's last finished decision, once (on the engine loop)."""
 150:         rec, self._decision = self._decision, None
 151:         return rec
 152: 
 153:     def take_plan_orders(self) -> list[AIOrder] | None:
 154:         """The last day plan's orders, once (on the engine loop); None: no new plan."""
 155:         orders, self._plan_orders = self._plan_orders, None
 156:         return orders
 157: 
 158:     def frames(self) -> kn.Frames:
 159:         """The closed bars of every frame the readers need, from the engine's candle service."""
 160:         get = {
 161:             tf: to_frame(self.candles.closed_candles(self.cfg.symbol, tf, n).df) for tf, n in COUNTS.items()
 162:         }
 163:         return kn.Frames(
 164:             get[Timeframe.M1], get[Timeframe.M15], get[Timeframe.H1], get[Timeframe.H4], get[Timeframe.D1],
 165:             self.releases,
 166:         )  # fmt: skip
 167: 
 168:     def close(self) -> None:
 169:         if self._executor is not None:
 170:             self._executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
 171: 
 172:     # --- the watcher's callables (the background thread) ---
 173: 
 174:     def _current_frames(self) -> kn.Frames:
 175:         if self._frames is None:
 176:             raise RuntimeError("no bars fetched yet")
 177:         return self._frames
 178: 
 179:     def _journal(self) -> str:
 180:         text = self.journal_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if self.journal_path.exists() else ""
 181:         return f"{text}\n\n{self.position}"  # at the end: the journal's tail keeps it
 182: 
 183:     def _plan(self, as_of: pd.Timestamp, kind: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 184:         rec = day_plan.make_plan(
 185:             self.provider, kn.load(), self._current_frames(), as_of, kind,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
 186:             model=self.cfg.model, store=self.store, journal=self.journal_path,
 187:         )  # fmt: skip
 188:         self._plan_orders = day_plan.plan_orders(rec)  # placed in advance (owner 2026-10-25)
 189:         return rec
 190: 
 191:     def _suspensions(self) -> dict[str, str]:
 192:         path = self.store / "suspended.json"
 193:         rows: dict[str, str] = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if path.exists() else {}
 194:         return rows
 195: 
 196:     def _decide(
 197:         self, as_of: pd.Timestamp, text: str, model: str | None, effort: str = trader.EFFORT
 198:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 199:         return trader.decide(
 200:             self.provider, kn.load(), self._current_frames(), as_of, text,
 201:             model=model, suspensions=self._suspensions(), store=self.store, source="watcher", effort=effort,
 202:         )  # fmt: skip
 203: 
 204: 
 205: def order_from(rec: dict[str, Any], pending_minutes: int) -> AIOrder | None:
 206:     """An AI decision record as an order for the ``ai_trader`` strategy: a SELL / BUY with a size the brakes
 207:     left ('half' / 'full') and its entry and stop; else None."""
 208:     d = rec.get("decision") or {}
 209:     size = rec.get("final_size")
 210:     side = d.get("decision") if d.get("decision") in ("SELL", "BUY") else rec.get("order_side")
 211:     if side not in ("SELL", "BUY") or size not in ("half", "full"):
 212:         return None
 213:     if d.get("entry") is None or d.get("stop") is None:
 214:         return None
 215:     at = pd.Timestamp(rec["as_of"]).to_pydatetime()
 216:     return AIOrder(
 217:         side=Side.SELL if side == "SELL" else Side.BUY,
 218:         entry=float(d["entry"]),
 219:         stop=float(d["stop"]),
 220:         target=None if d.get("target") is None else float(d["target"]),
 221:         risk_scale=0.5 if size == "half" else 1.0,
 222:         setup=str(d.get("setup", "")),
 223:         reasons=tuple(str(x) for x in d.get("reasons", [])),
 224:         kb_ids=tuple(str(x) for x in d.get("kb_ids", [])),
 225:         confidence=int(d.get("confidence") or 0),
 226:         decided_at=at,
 227:         valid_until=at + timedelta(minutes=max(1, min(240, int(d.get("valid_minutes") or pending_minutes)))),
 228:         key=f"step-{pd.Timestamp(rec['as_of']):%Y%m%d%H%M}",
 229:         watch_above=None if d.get("watch_above") is None else float(d["watch_above"]),
 230:         watch_below=None if d.get("watch_below") is None else float(d["watch_below"]),
 231:         exit_level=None if d.get("exit_level") is None else float(d["exit_level"]),
 232:         direct=d.get("decision") in ("SELL", "BUY"),
 233:     )
 234: 
 235: 
 236: def hand_over(strategy: AITrader, symbol: str, rec: dict[str, Any], pending_minutes: int) -> str:
 237:     """What the strategy does with the AI's decision: a new order (it replaces a waiting one), an exit of the
 238:     lane's open position, or a WAIT that withdraws a waiting order. Returns what was done."""
 239:     d = rec.get("decision") or {}
 240:     if rec.get("map_stops_now"):  # the plan's orders against the map's stops now (C4 at the fill)
 241:         strategy.recheck_plan_stops(symbol, rec["map_stops_now"])
 242:     for side, vetoes in (rec.get("side_vetoes") or {}).items():  # a rule in force stops a side now (R01 ...)
 243:         if vetoes:
 244:             strategy.drop_plan_side(symbol, Side.SELL if side == "SELL" else Side.BUY)
 245:     order = order_from(rec, pending_minutes)
 246:     if order is not None:
 247:         strategy.accept(symbol, order)
 248:         return "order"
 249:     if d.get("decision") == "EXIT":
 250:         strategy.request_exit(symbol)
 251:         return "exit"
 252:     if d.get("decision") in ("SELL", "BUY"):  # an entry the brakes vetoed withdraws the order it replaced
 253:         strategy.cancel(symbol)
 254:         return "cancel"
 255:     return "none"  # a WAIT keeps a waiting order (07-06: the 07:05 WAIT withdrew the 07:00 sell at 4,197.50)

 124:     def _modify(self, pos: BrokerPosition, spec: SymbolSpec, new_sl: float, mark: float, note: str) -> None:
 125:         if (new_sl - pos.sl) * pos.side.sign <= 0:
 126:             log.error("refused an unfavourable stop move on #%s (%s -> %s)", pos.ticket, pos.sl, new_sl)
 127:             return
 128:         if (mark - new_sl) * pos.side.sign <= spec.stops_level * spec.point:
 129:             return  # inside the stops level: the server would refuse it; try again on a later quote
 130:         frozen = spec.freeze_level * spec.point
 131:         if frozen and any(level and abs(mark - level) <= frozen for level in (pos.sl, pos.tp)):
 132:             return  # the current stop or target is frozen
 133:         now = self.clock.now_utc()
 134:         last = self._last_modify.get(pos.ticket)
 135:         if last is not None and (now - last).total_seconds() < self.config.modify_min_interval_seconds:
 136:             return
 137:         self._last_modify[pos.ticket] = now
 138:         result = self.orders.execution.send(
 139:             self.orders.builder.modify_stops(spec, pos, new_sl, pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None)
 140:         )
 141:         if result.ok or result.retcode == 10025:
 142:             if self.bus is not None:
 143:                 self.bus.emit(
 144:                     EventType.STOP_MOVED,
 145:                     symbol=pos.symbol,
 146:                     ticket=pos.ticket,
 147:                     old=pos.sl,
 148:                     new=new_sl,
 149:                     note=note,
 150:                     paper=False,
 151:                 )
 152:         else:
 153:             log.warning("stop change on #%s refused: %s", pos.ticket, result.description)
 154:             self.orders.monitor.observe_order_failure(f"modify #{pos.ticket}: {result.description}")

 158:     def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
 159:         spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
 160:         if spec is None:
 161:             return

 164:             if strategy is not None and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
 165:                 self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.SIGNAL)

 167:     def on_lane_bar(
 168:         self,
 169:         symbol: str,
 170:         ctx: StrategyContext,
 171:         strategies: Collection[str],
 172:         close_check: bool = False,
 173:     ) -> None:
 174:         """A lane's closed entry bar: the own management of its strategies. The position's signal is not
 175:         known here, so the strategy reads its plan from the position (``OpenPosition.signal_id`` empty).
 176:         With ``close_check`` the strategy is also asked to close: an extra lane never reaches
 177:         :meth:`on_bar`, so its daily flatten would otherwise never fire (review 2026-10-27)."""
 178:         spec = self.specs.get(symbol)
 179:         if spec is None:
 180:             return
 181:         initial = self.initial_stops()
 182:         for pos in self.bot_positions(symbol):
 183:             strategy = self.strategies_by_magic.get(pos.magic)
 184:             if strategy is None or strategy.name not in strategies:
 185:                 continue
 186:             if close_check and strategy.should_close(ctx, pos.side):
 187:                 self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.SIGNAL)
 188:                 continue
 189:             first_sl = initial.get(pos.ticket)
 190:             if first_sl is None or pos.sl <= 0:
 191:                 continue
 192:             mark = ctx.market.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else ctx.market.ask
 193:             if mark is None:
 194:                 continue
 195:             tp = pos.tp if pos.tp > 0 else None
 196:             view = OpenPosition(pos.side, pos.price_open, first_sl, pos.sl, tp, pos.time_utc)
 197:             adj = from_advice(strategy.manage_position(ctx, view), pos.side, mark=mark, sl=pos.sl)
 198:             if adj is None:
 199:                 continue
 200:             if adj.close is not None:
 201:                 self.close(pos, spec, adj.close)
 202:             elif adj.new_sl is not None:
 203:                 self._modify(pos, spec, adj.new_sl, mark, adj.note)
 204: 
 205:     # --- closing ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 206: 
 207:     def close(self, pos: BrokerPosition, spec: SymbolSpec, reason: ExitReason) -> SendResult:
 208:         tick = self.orders.market.tick(pos.symbol)
 209:         price = pos.price_current if tick is None else (tick.bid if pos.side is Side.BUY else tick.ask)
 210:         result = self.orders.execution.send(
 211:             self.orders.builder.close(spec, pos, price, comment=f"taa:{reason.value.lower()}")
 212:         )
 213:         if result.ok:
 214:             if self.bus is not None:
 215:                 self.bus.emit(
 216:                     EventType.POSITION_CLOSED,
 217:                     symbol=pos.symbol,
 218:                     ticket=pos.ticket,
 219:                     reason=reason.value,
 220:                     paper=False,
 221:                 )
 222:         else:
 223:             log.error("close of #%s (%s) failed: %s", pos.ticket, reason.value, result.description)
 224:             self.orders.monitor.observe_order_failure(f"close #{pos.ticket}: {result.description}")
 225:         return result
 226: 
 227:     def flatten(self, why: str) -> list[SendResult]:
 228:         """Close every bot position (kill switch FLATTEN). Refused unless explicitly allowed."""
 229:         if not self.flatten_allowed:
 230:             raise SafetyViolation("FLATTEN needs KILL_SWITCH_FLATTEN_ALLOWED=true")
 231:         results = []
 232:         for pos in self.bot_positions():
 233:             spec = self.specs.get(pos.symbol)
 234:             if spec is None:
 235:                 log.error("cannot flatten #%s: no spec for %s", pos.ticket, pos.symbol)
 236:                 continue
 237:             results.append(self.close(pos, spec, ExitReason.KILL_SWITCH))
 238:         log.critical("flatten (%s): %d close(s) sent", why, len(results))
 239:         return results

 458:     def _build_backend(self, account: Any, by_magic: dict[int, Any]) -> int:
 459:         """PAPER: the persisted paper book. DEMO / LIVE: orders, reconciliation and management on the broker
 460:         account (the same code path; LIVE only behind its gate)."""
 461:         cfg, env = self.config, self.settings.env
 462:         self._currency = str(account.currency)
 463:         if self.settings.mode is TradingMode.PAPER:
 464:             self.paper = PaperExecution(
 465:                 self.db,
 466:                 self.account_key,
 467:                 self.symbols,
 468:                 LiveRates(self.gateway, account.currency),
 469:                 self.clock,
 470:                 paper=cfg.paper,
 471:                 backtest=cfg.backtest,
 472:                 account_currency=account.currency,
 473:                 leverage=float(account.leverage or cfg.backtest.leverage),
 474:                 starting_equity=account.equity,
 475:                 bus=self.bus,
 476:                 limit_deadline=self._limit_deadline,
 477:             )
 478:             restored = self.paper.restore()
 479:             self.positions = PositionManager(
 480:                 self.paper,
 481:                 cfg.management,
 482:                 self.symbols,
 483:                 self.clock,
 484:                 strategies_by_magic=by_magic,
 485:                 bus=self.bus,
 486:             )
 487:             self.backend: Backend = PaperBackend(self.paper, self.positions, self.gateway, self.kill_switch)
 488:             return restored
 489:         deviation = cfg.execution.deviation_points
 490:         self.orders = OrderManager(
 491:             self.db,

 493:             self.gateway,
 494:             self.monitor,

 502:         )

 506:             self.gateway,
 507:             self.monitor,
 508:             self.clock,
 509:             cfg.execution,

 127: def test_h4_is_built_from_h1_open_times_and_only_from_closed_bars(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
 128:     # Found in review 2026-10-27: dropping the close-to-open shift, or letting an unclosed H4 bar in, left
 129:     # every test green. App H1 frames are stamped at the close, so an H1 bar stamped 04:00 covers 03:00-04:00
 130:     # and belongs to the 00:00-04:00 H4 bar, not to 04:00-08:00.

 132:     h1 = pd.DataFrame({"open": 100.0, "high": [101.0, 102, 103, 104, 105, 106, 107, 108],

 143: def h4_highs_seen(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, at: datetime, highs: list[float]) -> list[float]:
 144:     """Run evaluate() with 1,200 H1 bars closing at ``at``'s hour and capture what the ZigZag is given."""
 145:     ctx = context()
 146:     closes = pd.date_range(end=at.replace(minute=0), periods=1200, freq="1h")
 147:     h1 = pd.DataFrame({"open": 90.0, "high": highs, "low": 80.0, "close": 90.0}, index=closes)
 148:     m5 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M5).copy()
 149:     m5.index = pd.DatetimeIndex([at - timedelta(minutes=5), at])
 150:     m1 = ctx.frame(Timeframe.M1).copy()
 151:     m1.index = pd.date_range(at - timedelta(minutes=60), at, freq="1min", tz="UTC")
 152:     later = StrategyContext(
 153:         replace(ctx.market, decision_time_utc=at),
 154:         {Timeframe.M1: m1, Timeframe.M5: m5, Timeframe.H1: h1},
 155:         at + timedelta(seconds=3),
 156:     )
 157:     seen: list[float] = []
 158: 
 159:     def capture(high, _low):
 160:         seen.extend(float(x) for x in high)
 161:         return flat_swings(), None
 162: 
 163:     monkeypatch.setattr(module, "zigzag", capture)
 164:     RangeBreak().evaluate(later)
 165:     return seen

> app\indicators\common.py:60:def recursive_smooth(values: FloatArray, n: int, alpha: float) -> FloatArray:
  app\indicators\common.py:61:    """``y[t] = alpha * x[t] + (1 - alpha) * y[t-1]``, seeded with the mean of the first 
full window.
  app\indicators\common.py:62:
  app\indicators\common.py:63:    ``alpha = 2 / (n + 1)`` gives the EMA, ``alpha = 1 / n`` gives Wilder's smoothing 
(RMA).
  app\indicators\common.py:64:    """
  app\indicators\common.py:65:    out = np.full(values.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)
  app\indicators\common.py:66:    seed_at = first_full_window(values, n)
  app\indicators\common.py:67:    if seed_at is None:
  app\indicators\common.py:68:        return out
  app\indicators\common.py:69:    prev = float(np.mean(values[seed_at - n + 1 : seed_at + 1]))
  app\indicators\common.py:70:    out[seed_at] = prev
  app\indicators\common.py:71:    keep = 1.0 - alpha
  app\indicators\common.py:72:    for i in range(seed_at + 1, len(values)):
  app\indicators\common.py:73:        prev = alpha * float(values[i]) + keep * prev
  app\indicators\common.py:74:        out[i] = prev
  app\indicators\common.py:75:    return out

"""Confirmed-swing trend pullbacks with a closed M5 range break."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import time
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import pandas as pd
from pydantic import Field

from app.core.enums import Action, ExitReason, Side, Timeframe
from app.indicators.volatility import atr
from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy, StrategyParams
from app.strategy.edge_side import edge_side
from app.strategy.signal_models import (
    Condition,
    OpenPosition,
    PositionAdvice,
    ReasonCode,
    Signal,
    StrategyContext,
)
from app.strategy.zigzag import Swing, zigzag

BANGKOK = ZoneInfo("Asia/Bangkok")
NEW_YORK = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")


class TrendBoxParams(StrategyParams):
    """Measured constants shared by the two trend directions."""

    buffer: float = Field(default=0.20, ge=0, le=2)
    min_r: float = Field(default=1.2, gt=0, le=10)
    # Owner 2026-10-27: "you can take the 5 USD out". Measured first (research/.../gate_bite.py): the
    # 5.00 USD floor rejected 1 of 496 complete signals, because every target here is a swing or half an
    # H4 box. The gate stays so a lane can set one; 0.0 is off.
    min_take_profit: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0)
    # The measured default differs per direction, so each subclass sets its own; a lever either way
    # (journal 2026-10-27: a down trade reached +1.40 R and still stopped out at -1.01 R).
    break_even: bool = False
    require_big_tf: bool = False
    require_edge_side: bool = False
    min_stop_atr: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0)


class TrendBoxDownParams(TrendBoxParams):
    box: float = Field(default=0.20, gt=0, le=1)


class TrendBoxUpParams(TrendBoxParams):
    box: float = Field(default=0.33, gt=0, le=1)
    break_even: bool = True


def in_window(at: pd.Timestamp) -> bool:
    """Research windows use a start-exclusive, end-inclusive close timestamp."""
    utc = at.tz_convert("UTC")
    bangkok = utc.tz_convert(BANGKOK)
    new_york = utc.tz_convert(NEW_YORK)
    bt, nt = bangkok.time(), new_york.time()
    return (time(7) < bt <= time(9)) or (time(13) < bt <= time(15)) or (time(8) < nt <= time(11, 30))


def stepped(swings: list[Swing]) -> str:
    highs = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == 1]
    lows = [s.price for s in swings if s.kind == -1]
    if len(highs) < 2 or len(lows) < 2:
        return "FLAT"
    if highs[-1] < highs[-2] and lows[-1] < lows[-2]:
        return "DOWN"
    if highs[-1] > highs[-2] and lows[-1] > lows[-2]:
        return "UP"
    return "FLAT"


"""H4 confirmed-swing range breaks on M5 closes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import time
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import pandas as pd
from pydantic import Field

from app.core.enums import Action, Side, Timeframe
from app.strategy.base_strategy import BaseStrategy, StrategyParams
from app.strategy.edge_side import edge_side
from app.strategy.signal_models import (
    Condition,
    OpenPosition,
    PositionAdvice,
    ReasonCode,
    Signal,
    StrategyContext,
)
from app.strategy.trend_box import break_even_advice, in_window, running_push_exceeded, stepped
from app.strategy.zigzag import zigzag

BANGKOK = ZoneInfo("Asia/Bangkok")


class RangeBreakParams(StrategyParams):
    """Measured breakout plan constants."""

    buffer: float = Field(default=0.3, ge=0, le=2)
    tp_range: float = Field(default=0.5, gt=0, le=5)
    min_r: float = Field(default=0.8, gt=0, le=10)
    # Owner 2026-10-27: "you can take the 5 USD out". Measured first (research/.../gate_bite.py): the
    # 5.00 USD floor rejected 1 of 496 complete signals, because every target here is a swing or half an
    # H4 box. The gate stays so a lane can set one; 0.0 is off.
    min_take_profit: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0)
    require_edge_side: bool = False


class RangeBreak(BaseStrategy):
    name = "range_break"
    Params = RangeBreakParams

    def required_timeframes(self) -> tuple[Timeframe, ...]:
        return Timeframe.M1, Timeframe.M5, Timeframe.H1

    def warmup_bars(self) -> int:
        # H1 bars, not H4: the 300 H4 bars the box is read from are resampled from 1,200 closed H1 bars.
        # Declaring 300 here would let a lane configure 300 H1 bars, give the ZigZag 75 H4 bars instead of
        # 300, and change the box silently (found in review, 2026-10-27).
        return 1200

    @staticmethod
    def _h4_from_h1(h1: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        # App frames are indexed by H1 close; the research resamples bars indexed by H1 open.
        opens = h1.copy()
        opens.index = pd.DatetimeIndex(opens.index) - pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
                    old=old,
                    new=adj.new_sl,
                    kind=None if adj.stop_kind is None else adj.stop_kind.value,
                    note=adj.note,
                    paper=True,
                )

    def on_bar(self, symbol: str, ctx: StrategyContext) -> None:
        """A new closed entry bar: count it for the time stop and ask the owning strategy about closing."""
        for pos in [p for p in self.paper.broker.positions.values() if p.symbol == symbol]:
"""Shared building blocks for indicators: input coercion, rolling means and recursive smoothers.

Conventions shared by every indicator (see docs/INDICATORS.md):

- Inputs are pandas Series; outputs keep the input index and use ``float64``.
- The value at position *t* depends only on positions <= *t* (no look-ahead).
- Warm-up positions are NaN. Callers treat NaN as "insufficient data" (HOLD).
- Recursive smoothers seed with the simple mean of the first *n* consecutive finite inputs, so leading NaNs
  (the warm-up of an upstream indicator) are skipped. A NaN *after* the seed propagates to every later value:
  validated candles never contain NaN, so one appearing means the data is broken and the output fails closed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt
import pandas as pd

from app.core.errors import ConfigError, DataQualityError

FloatArray = npt.NDArray[np.float64]


def check_period(name: str, value: int, minimum: int = 1) -> None:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int | np.integer) or value < minimum:
        raise ConfigError(f"{name} must be an integer >= {minimum} (got {value!r})")


def as_float(series: pd.Series) -> FloatArray:
    return series.to_numpy(dtype=np.float64, na_value=np.nan)


def check_aligned(*series: pd.Series) -> None:
    """Multi-input indicators (high/low/close) require series of one frame, not mixed sources."""
    first = series[0]
    for other in series[1:]:
        if len(other) != len(first) or not other.index.equals(first.index):
            raise DataQualityError("indicator inputs must share the same index")


def to_series(values: FloatArray, like: pd.Series, name: str) -> pd.Series:
    return pd.Series(values, index=like.index, name=name, dtype=np.float64)


def rolling_mean(values: FloatArray, n: int) -> FloatArray:
    """Simple moving average; NaN until *n* values are available and wherever the window holds a NaN."""
    return pd.Series(values).rolling(n, min_periods=n).mean().to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)


def first_full_window(values: FloatArray, n: int) -> int | None:
    """Index of the last element of the first window of *n* consecutive finite values."""
    run = 0
    for i, v in enumerate(values):
        run = run + 1 if np.isfinite(v) else 0
        if run >= n:
            return i
    return None


def recursive_smooth(values: FloatArray, n: int, alpha: float) -> FloatArray:

I found a point-in-time issue in the research path: `to_frame()` indexes candles by their open time, but the momentum and regime scripts subtract an hour as if those timestamps were close times. I’m checking the exact resulting availability timestamps and the remaining close ownership paths before I finalize the report.

# Review report

## 1. Measurement scripts

**Verdict: point-in-time correctness fails in `momentum.py` and `edge_by_condition.py`.** The scripts shift H1 timestamps back one hour as if they are close times, but [to_frame()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/app/engine/ai_trader.py:67>) documents and implements an index of **bar open times**.

- In [momentum.py](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/momentum.py:59>), subtracting an hour and then setting `readable = f.index + hours` makes a source H1 bar readable at its **open**, an hour before it closes. I checked this with a synthetic frame using `.venv\Scripts\python.exe -B`: a bar opening at 11:00 UTC closes at 12:00, but its reported `readable` time is 11:00. `read_at()` accepts that timestamp at line 70, so it can return an unfinished bar. The same one-hour shift misaligns H4 and D1 resampling; for example, a D1 bucket can include the next date’s 00:00-open H1 bar while claiming to be closed at 00:00.
- In [edge_by_condition.py](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/edge_by_condition.py:47>), the shift likewise assigns the open-stamped H1 bars to the wrong D1 buckets. `closed_at` then understates when the final included source bar is actually available. This can label a trade using a regime built from a bar that had not closed.

**`edge_by_condition.regimes()` also does not implement the stated bear definition exactly.** At [line 55](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/edge_by_condition.py:55>), `~over & ~rising` treats “at or under the mean” and “not rising” as bear. That includes equality and a flat mean; the brief defines bear as strictly below a falling mean. Those cases should be mixed.

**The momentum ATR is Wilder-smoothed, but its seed does not match the app’s Wilder/MT5 implementation.** [momentum.py:54](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/momentum.py:54>) uses `ewm(adjust=False, min_periods=n)`, which recursively seeds from the first TR and merely hides early outputs. The app’s [recursive_smooth()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/app/indicators/common.py:60>) seeds with the mean of the first full window, then applies Wilder smoothing. The ATR values—and therefore momentum bins and correlations—can differ, especially near warmup or after truncated history.

**The geometry counterfactual is correct.** In [path_stats()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/geometry.py:48>), each widened stop uses the same entry and target, and settles through `day_end(t.at)` at line 66. `bots.settle()` also applies the same `max_hours` limit. It does not stop at the original exit time. Multiplying `got.r` by `k` is correct: `got.r` is net gain after spread divided by `k × original risk`, so multiplying by `k` expresses the result in original-risk units. The x1 result is checked against the original trade at lines 117–121.

For the other geometry timestamps, `bots.run()` uses open-stamped history consistently: it includes H1 bars only when `index + 1 hour <= t_close` at [bots.py:211](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/bots.py:211>), and resamples those open-stamped bars for H4 at line 195. The entry and exit paths use M1 bars indexed by open time. I found no corresponding lookahead in that path.

**The fit/unseen presentation still mixes the halves.** Geometry prints fitted and unseen separately. But [edge_by_condition.cells()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/edge_by_condition.py:82>) reports win rate, R per trade, and total R on the combined group, alongside only the two R means. [momentum.bins()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/momentum.py:89>) does the same, and its correlations at lines 99–101 use the full sample. Those aggregate claims can be carried by the fitted half even when the unseen half differs; the displayed split means do not show each half’s sample count.

## 2. Shipped-parameter guard

**Verdict: the guard catches one specific call, but it does not close the parameter mismatch hole.**

[bots.run()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/bots.py:186>) rejects a `P` equal to `P()`. `replace(P())` with no changes is also equal to `P()` and is rejected. But the guard does not validate dict contents: `{"DOWN": P()}` or a dict with any other incorrect parameters bypasses it. The existing guard tests cover bare defaults and an intentional variation, but not a wrong dict.

`audit.BEST is bots.SHIPPED` ensures those two names share one object; it does not validate the object against the app.

The app comparison test checks DOWN/UP `box`, `buffer`, and `min_r`, plus RANGE `buffer`, `tp_range`, and `min_r` in [test_bots.py:45](</C:/Users/korap/taa/research/2026-10-26-three-bots/test_bots.py:45>). A change to one of those checked app defaults would fail the test. A change to an unchecked app default—such as either strategy’s `min_take_profit`—would not. The test also does not compare `range_tf` to the app’s H4 input. I did not change defaults to run a mutation test; the assertions establish exactly which fields are covered.

## 3. Engine close handling

**Verdict: extra-lane close checks run, but `on_bar()` can still close a position from a different lane.**

The orchestrator calls `backend.on_bar()` on the main lane, then calls `on_lane_bar()` with `close_check=False` for MAIN and `True` for extra lanes at [orchestrator.py:1135](</C:/Users/korap/taa/app/engine/orchestrator.py:1135>). That avoids asking a main-lane strategy’s `should_close()` twice in the same cycle.

However, `on_bar()` does not filter positions by lane. Both [PositionManager.on_bar()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/app/engine/position_manager.py:118>) and [BrokerPositionManager.on_bar()](</C:/Users/korap/taa/app/engine/broker_positions.py:158>) ask every position’s owning strategy to close using the **main lane’s** context. An extra-lane position can therefore be closed on the main lane’s bar if its strategy’s `should_close()` returns true for that context. The lane ownership test only exercises `on_lane_bar()` with the wrong strategy-name list; it does not cover this `on_bar()` path.

I found no normal sequential double-close in the loop itself: `bot_positions()` returns a list snapshot, each entry is visited once, and a successful close should disappear from the next broker-position query. But the broker manager has no `close_requested` or per-ticket deduplication state, so it relies on the broker no longer returning a successfully closed position when the subsequent lane callback queries again. The loop is safe from list mutation; idempotence is not independently enforced.

## 4. H4 cutoff tests

**Verdict: the pair now covers both sides of the cutoff, and the helper does not duplicate the production cutoff logic.**

The unclosed-bar test places a sentinel high in the still-forming H4 bar and checks it never reaches the ZigZag. The newest-closed-bar test places a different sentinel in the latest completed H4 bar and checks it does reach the ZigZag. Together they cover excluding a forming bar and retaining the newest closed one.

`h4_highs_seen()` builds the test context and captures the input passed to the ZigZag; it calls `RangeBreak.evaluate()` to exercise the production filter. It does not reimplement that filter.

## Change I would most want

Fix the H1 timestamp assumption in `momentum.py` and `edge_by_condition.py` first. Their current one-hour shift can make unfinished-bar data appear readable, so the momentum and regime conclusions are not point-in-time safe. No code was changed, and I printed this report here rather than writing a findings file.
